## R6-1 · 🔴 THE FINDING THAT CHANGES THE GATE: `verify-cutover.ts` CANNOT SEE ITS OWN COPY

**Measured, not reasoned.** A verification agent replaced `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` with
`[]` in a sandboxed copy of the tree, ran every booking script against baseline
and broken, and deleted the sandbox. **The repo was never touched.**

| Script | Baseline | With `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS = []` | `✗` printed | Summary line |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `verify-booking-pricing` | exit 0 | **exit 1** | 1 | **none — crashed at `:76`** |
| `verify-booking-email` | exit 0 | **exit 1** | 5 | **none — crashed at `:592`** |
| `verify-booking-ics` | exit 0 | **exit 1** | **0** | **none — crashed at `:791`** |
| **`verify-cutover`** | exit 0 | ✅ **exit 0** | 0 | ✅ **`✓ cutover checks passed`** |
| `tsc --noEmit --strict` | — | **exit 0** | — | typecheck stays **green** |

🔴 **`verify-cutover.ts` — the WEB gate, this ticket's own gate — stays FULLY
GREEN while `$399`, `$699` and `$1,199` vanish from `dist/client/book/index.html`
and `dist/client/index.html`.**

**Why:** every terms pin in that file matches the **identifier name**, not the
value. `TERMS_CONSTANTS` (`:350-357`) is six **strings**: `'FEE_TERMS_HEADING'`,
`'FEE_TERMS_INTRO'`, … `checkTermsOrder` (`:363-372`), the presence loop
(`:406-407`), the CTA/form-arm loop (`:445-453`) and the island loop (`:536-537`)
all run `code.includes('<NAME>')`. **The templates still name the constant, so
everything passes.** There is no `399`, `699` or `1,199` anywhere in
`verify-cutover.ts`.

⚠️ **So revision 5's premise — that BK-53's copy edits are pinned by the web
gate — is false in the direction that matters.** The web gate pins that a
constant is *referenced*, in the right *order*, in the right *arm*. It pins
nothing about what the constant *says*.

**→ R6-3 adds VALUE-level pins to `verify-cutover.ts`, and that is the single
biggest change in this revision.**

### And three scripts crash rather than fail

- **`verify-booking-ics.ts` exits 1 with ZERO `✗` and no summary line.**
  **CLAUDE.md's BK-33 trap, live, in this repo, on this ticket's own edit.** A
  harness measuring `grep -c "✗"` reads it as a clean pass.
- `verify-booking-pricing.ts` prints one `✗` then throws at `:76`, so
  **`:83-87`'s `formatCents` and `+ GST` pins never run.**
- `tsconfig.json` extends `astro/tsconfigs/strict`, which sets only
  `"strict": true` — **no `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`** — so `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS[0]`
  types as `string` on an empty array and **every one of those crashes is
  invisible to `npm run typecheck`.**

⚠️ **This corrects R5-12's BL-7 in both directions.** BL-7 said the length zip is
*"the only assertion in the repo that reddens when `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` is
emptied"*. **It is not** — six named `✗` fire across two scripts. **But BL-7's
instruction stands and is now better founded: keep the zip**, because it is the
one assertion that fails *cleanly* before the crashes start. **And every
red-first row touching this array must score on `(exit code, summary line)`, never
on a `✗` count** — one of the three scripts prints no marker at all.

## R6-2 · THE COPY — **replaces R5-4 entirely**

⚠️ **Decision 25 is absolute: no number on any web surface.** Every draft is
checked against it, and R6-3's `WEB_AMOUNT_BAN` enforces it.

### The terms block — **and it gets a NAME (BL-3)**

🔴 **The constant is `ASSESSMENT_TERMS`, a new export in `booking-copy.ts`.**
It **replaces** `FEE_TERMS_INTRO`, `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT`, `FEE_TERMS_REFUND` and
`FEE_TERMS_CREDIT`. `FEE_TERMS_HEADING`, `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` and
`FEE_TERMS_ACK_LABEL` survive under their own names.

*(R5-12 BL-3: revision 5 never named it, so `TERMS_CONSTANTS` could not be
re-pointed and the coupling pin's colour was the implementer's coin-flip. Naming
it is what makes R6-3 writable. And per R5-11 A17: **do not** reuse
`FEE_TERMS_REFUND` for five non-refund sentences — a constant whose name lies
about its contents is how the next reader gets it wrong.)*

```ts
export const ASSESSMENT_TERMS: readonly string[] = [
  'The on-site assessment visit is free.',
  'If your address is outside our service area, a travel fee may apply. We tell you the amount when we confirm your appointment.',
  'You get our findings in person at the visit. If you want to go ahead, we follow up with a quote — that is where any report options and prices are.',
  'There is no obligation to go ahead.',
  'We never take money without telling you the amount first — any charge is a link you choose to pay.',
  'If we ever ask you to pay for something before the visit, we tell you the amount and the date. If it is not paid by then we may release the time — and you are welcome to book again.',
  CANCEL_LINE,
];
```

**Seven items. Item 6 is R5-10 T2's slot-release pre-consent** — the only
statement, anywhere the customer sees before consenting, that a slot can be
released; R3-SF12 showed `APPROVED_*` cannot carry it because that email *is* the
one asking for money. **Item 7 is `CANCEL_LINE` itself, not a copy** — the same
identity `FEE_TERMS_REFUND[4]` had (`booking-copy.ts:509`), so its two email
renders and its web render stay one string.

⚠️ **Item 5 is TRUE TODAY:** `booking-payment.ts:374` uses `mode: 'payment'` with
no `setup_future_usage` and no stored customer (R3-BL-6).
⚠️ **Item 5 deliberately avoids the words `nothing is charged` / `no charge` /
`not charged` / `nothing to pay`** — all four are members of R6-3's
`FREE_CLAIM_SHAPES`, and `verify-booking-email.ts:377` positively *requires* one
of them in `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT` today. **Deleting that array dissolves the
collision; wording item 5 with those words would rebuild it** (R5-11 A6).

### `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` — figures stripped, array **KEPT**, name kept

🔴 **DO NOT DELETE (R3-BL-2), and DO NOT EMPTY (R6-1).**

1. *"The assessment visit — a technician walks the affected areas and talks you through what they find. **Free.**"*
2. *"A written cause-of-loss report **and a repair estimate**: the documentation your adjuster works from if you are filing a claim. **Quoted with your job.**"*
3. *"A measured sketch **and diagram** of the affected areas, alongside the report **and estimate**. **Quoted with your job.** Where lab samples are taken, **results take 3–5 business days**."*

⚠️ *"and estimate"* / *"and diagram"* are restored deliberately — dropping them
is the exact edit BK-36 made and plan review reverted (R3-SF3).
⚠️ **It is the ONLY path by which the 3–5 day lab turnaround reaches any email**
(BK-55 G9 pins that).
⚠️ **The length zip in `verify-booking-pricing.ts:68-71` stays** and keeps this
array at one bullet per tier.

### 🔴 R6-2a · W17 REVERSED — the tier field stays REQUIRED

**Revision 5 said make it optional. Three findings say do not.**

1. **C-j: it is enforced in TWO files, and only one was named.**
   `booking-form.ts:273-275` is the **client** validator, imported by
   `BookingForm.svelte`. The server's 422 comes from
   **`booking-payload.ts:277-286`** via `create.ts:73`, and it fires **only when
   `options.entry === 'public'`** — which is why admin rows may carry no tier.
2. **It is a change to the public WRITE PATH**, not copy. This ticket is
   **Reviewed**, and its whole justification for that tier is that the email/money
   half left.
3. **Decision 18's own reasoning forbids it** (R5-12 SF-11): *"Removing a
   required field would itself be a funnel change, in the same window as the
   pricing flip — against the one-funnel-change-per-week rule."* Revision 5 kept
   the checkbox required on exactly that argument and then removed a different
   required field in the same commit.

**→ The legend does NOT say "(optional)". The field stays required. R3-SF9's
contradiction is resolved by fixing the COPY, which is what this ticket owns.**
Making it genuinely optional is a separate ticket if the client ever wants it.

## R6-3 · THE GATE — **replaces R5-5 entirely**

### 3a · The coupling pin — **over VALUES, and the pattern already exists**

> **A surface that claims the assessment is free, OR releases obligation, must
> carry the travel-fee qualifier. Claim and qualifier travel together, or red.**

🔴 **The unit is the JOINED TERMS TEXT, not the file and not the constant.**
R5-12 BL-2 proved the per-constant form is red on arrival —
`ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE` and `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS[0]` each carry a free claim and
neither can carry a qualifier without absurdity. BL-6 proved the whole-file form
is red on arrival too, for a different reason: **8 of 12 matches in
`booking-copy.ts` are in comments.**

**The joined-values unit is what a customer actually reads, and there is a
working precedent — `verify-booking-email.ts:283-289` already does exactly
this:**

```ts
const terms = [FEE_TERMS_INTRO, ...FEE_TERMS_ITEMS, ...FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT,
               ...FEE_TERMS_REFUND, FEE_TERMS_CREDIT].join(' ');
```

**→ `verify-cutover.ts` imports the values and builds the same shape:**

```ts
// The SECOND import from `src/` in this file. The first (:43) earned its place
// by finding the built chunk; this one earns its place because every terms pin
// below it matched an identifier NAME, and a sandboxed break proved the whole
// file stays green while the prices vanish from dist/. Names tell you a constant
// is referenced. Only the value tells you what it says.
import {
  ASSESSMENT_TERMS, ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE, ASSESSMENT_TIER_DESCRIPTIONS,
  FEE_TERMS_ACK_LABEL, FEE_TERMS_HEADING, FEE_TERMS_ITEMS,
} from '../src/lib/booking-copy';

const TERMS_TEXT = [
  FEE_TERMS_HEADING, ...ASSESSMENT_TERMS, ...FEE_TERMS_ITEMS,
  ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE, ...Object.values(ASSESSMENT_TIER_DESCRIPTIONS),
  FEE_TERMS_ACK_LABEL,
].join(' ');
```

```ts
const FREE_CLAIM_SHAPES = [
  /\bfree[^.<>]{0,40}?\b(?:visits?|assessments?)\b/i,
  /\b(?:visits?|assessments?)\b[^.<>]{0,40}?\b(?:is|are|'s|itself is)\s+free\b/i,
  /(?:^|[.>])\s*free\.\s*(?:$|[<'"`,])/im,        // R5-11 A7 — the trailing quote
  /\bno[\s-]+obligations?\b/i,                     // plural + reflow, BK-48
  /\bwithout\s+obligation\b/i,
  /\bno\s+(?:commitment|strings|cost|charge)\b/i,
  /\b(?:nothing is charged|not charged|nothing to pay|at no cost)\b/i,
  /\b(?:don'?t|do not|won'?t)\s+charge\b/i,
  /\b(?:pay nothing|costs? you nothing|complimentary|waived?)\b/i,
  /\$\s*0(?:\.00)?\b/,
];
const TRAVEL_QUALIFIER = /\btravel[\s-]+fees?\b/i;   // A10 + N-1: hyphen IS native — booking-copy.ts:447
```

**Assert: if any shape matches `TERMS_TEXT`, `TRAVEL_QUALIFIER` matches it too —
and assert BOTH sides are non-empty**, so the pin cannot pass by matching nothing.

⚠️ **The exempt question dissolves.** `NO_CHARGE_LINE`, `DECLINED_LEAD`,
`EXPIRED_REQUEST_LEAD` and `PAYMENT_EXPIRED_LEAD` are **not in `TERMS_TEXT`** —
they are email lifecycle copy, not terms. **R5-10 T3's exempt list is no longer
needed and is withdrawn**, which is strictly better: *an exemption is how a ban
dies quietly*, and this design has none.

### 3b · The rendered-surface twin — **a real block slice, not a whole file**

R5-11 A2 showed A6's source twin is unsatisfiable on `ContactSection.astro` and
satisfied by a `<meta>` tag on `book.astro`. **The eyebrow now carries its own
qualifier (R6-2), so the twin is writable — and it uses machinery that exists.**

**`verify-cutover.ts:784-791` is the pattern to copy** — a start+end slice that
**asserts the slice is locatable and non-empty before using it**:

```ts
const cardAt = islandRaw.indexOf('{#if result}');
const formAt = islandRaw.indexOf('{:else}');
check(cardAt !== -1 && formAt !== -1 && cardAt < formAt, '… the slice below is real');
const card = /* slice */; check(card.length > 0, 'and the card slice is non-empty');
```

**Assert, per surface:** the eyebrow's own line contains a free claim **and**
`TRAVEL_QUALIFIER` — as **literal text in the rendered template**, never a
constant name. ⚠️ **State in the pin's comment that
`code.includes('FEE_TERMS_INTRO')` does NOT satisfy it** (the trap
`verify-cutover.ts:328-332` already documents).

⚠️ **Do not slice the island's terms-box div for this.** Verified: `:1111-1218`
contains the tier fieldset, `AFTER_HOURS_NOTE`, three radio labels with
`formatCents(...) + GST` at `:1173`, `QUOTE_HEADING` and the Assessment/GST/Total
`<dl>`. **It is not a pure terms region** and a regex over it sees all of that.

| **`:471-477`** `!/no obligation/i` over `book.astro` + `ContactSection.astro`, **whole file incl. frontmatter** | 🔴 **RED** — the new meta description says *"no obligation"* in frontmatter | Re-scoped to the coupling form |
| **`:512-517`** `!/free\s+assessment/i`, **`templateOf` only** | ⚠️ **GREEN — and R5-5b said RED, wrongly (A8).** `\s+` does not span `on-site `. **Re-scope it because it is VACUOUS**, not because it fails | Coupling form |
| **`:1055-1071`** `BANNED_SHAPES` over `dist/client/**/*.{js,html}` | 🔴 **RED** — `/\bfree[^.<>]{0,40}?assessments?\b/i` matches *"Free on-site assessment"* in the two pages A5's twin **requires** to carry it | Coupling form; **`.js` excluded** (A3 — chunk co-location is a Vite decision) |

### 3d · 🔴 `WEB_AMOUNT_BAN` — decision 25 enforced, and the scope is **already computed**

R5-12 BL-5 showed the naive scope is red on arrival. **Verified empirically
against the real `dist/`:**

```
dist/client/book/index.html            $399, $699, $1,199   ← FEE_TERMS_ITEMS
dist/client/index.html                 $399, $699, $1,199   ← FEE_TERMS_ITEMS, CTA arm
dist/client/insurance-claims/index.html  $500, $2,500       ← unrelated, CORRECT copy
dist/client/_astro/booking-handoff.*.js  $399               ← the terms chunk
```

🔴 **And the decisive fact: `$150` appears in exactly TWO places in all of `src/`
on `bk51-gated`** — `[id].astro:621` (a comment) and `:1861` (rendered **admin**
markup) — **and reaches NO file in `dist/client`**, because every
`src/pages/admin/**` page carries `export const prerender = false;` and lands in
`dist/server`. **`dist/client` has no `admin` directory at all.**

**→ Scope it to `bookingSurfaces`, which `verify-cutover.ts:1130-1135` ALREADY
BUILDS** — `dist/client/book/**.html`, `dist/client/index.html`, plus
`termsBundles` (the `.js` chunks containing `TERMS_MARKER`). **That excludes
`insurance-claims` structurally rather than by a carve-out list**, which answers
R5-12's N-3: the carve-outs matched none of their own shapes because there was
nothing legitimate to carve out once the scope was right.

```ts
const WEB_AMOUNT_SHAPES = [
  /\$\s?[\d,]/,
  /\b\d[\d,]*\s*(?:dollars|CAD)\b/i,
  /\b(?:one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine)\s+hundred\b/i,
  /\b\d[\d,]*\s*(?:\+\s*GST|plus GST)\b/i,
];
```

**After this ticket the tier prices are gone, so this ban is GREEN on arrival and
stays green.** ⚠️ **It is red TODAY, deliberately** — that is red-first row 9, and
it must be observed red before the copy lands.
⚠️ **`SUPPORT_PHONE`, *"about 30 minutes"* and *"3–5 business days"* match none of
the four shapes** — verified — so ~~**no carve-out list exists at all**~~
⛔ **STRUCK IN PLACE 2026-09-06 (R7-A 🟠18 · R8-7 · R8-10 T7).** R7-4 added
`\b\d{3,}\b` with the year and the phone number excluded by name, so a carve-out
list existed from that moment and this sentence has been false since.
🔴 **R8-5b then retired the question: under the "values, never names" prose
corpus the ONLY carve-out is the support phone — and a `\d{4}` year carve-out is
FORBIDDEN BY NAME, because it is a hole the size of `$1,199`.** If one ever
becomes necessary, it needs a comment per entry and a non-growing assertion.

**Binding rules:**
- ⚠️ **DELETE constants; never EMPTY them** — R6-1 measured what emptying does.
- ⚠️ **Pin the orphaned readers' ABSENCE.** `noUnusedLocals` is not set
  (`verify-cutover.ts:558-559` records an orphaned import raising no error).

  is the pattern) — a whole-file ban on `booking-copy.ts` cannot exclude
  `PAYMENT_CANCELLED_LEAD`, which lives in it (R3B-C2).
- ⚠️ **`stripForUse`'s `//` rule is `/(^|[^:])\/\/[^\n]*/g`**, deliberately, so it
  does not eat `https://`. Any new source-level scan must use it and not a naive
  stripper — and note it does **not** run on `dist/`, where every amount is real.

  description matches `/quoted with your job/i` (R3B-D5).

# 🟦 REVISION 5 — THE WEB HALF. 2026-09-05. **THIS IS THE SPEC.**

**Written against decision 24's split.** This section is the implementable spec
for BK-53 as rescoped. It supersedes revisions 2, 3 and 4 **as scope**; those
sections stay only so a finding can be recovered by its ID.

**Target branch: `bk51-gated`.** Ships in ONE push with **BK-51 and BK-55**,
never separately. Decision 24 split the *review*, not the *deploy*.

## R5-1 · 🔴 THE SPLIT HAS A SEAM, AND IT RUNS STRAIGHT THROUGH THE TERMS BOX

**This is new. It is in no review, and it blocks both halves until it is settled.**

Decision 24's table gives **the terms box** to BK-53 and **`FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` as
it reaches email** to BK-55. **Those are the same constants.** Verified —
`booking-email.ts:37-75` imports `CANCEL_LINE`, `FEE_TERMS_CREDIT`,
`FEE_TERMS_HEADING`, `FEE_TERMS_INTRO`, `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS`, `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT`,
`FEE_TERMS_REFUND`, `RECEIVED_NEXT_STEPS` and `ASSESSMENT_TIER_NAMES`, and
renders them at:

| Constant | Web renders (BK-53's group) | Email renders (BK-55's group) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `FEE_TERMS_HEADING` | `book.astro`, `ContactSection.astro`, island | `booking-email.ts:522` (HTML), `:604` (text) |
| `FEE_TERMS_INTRO` | `book.astro:11`, `ContactSection.astro:347`, `BookingForm.svelte:1113` | `:524`, `:605` — **request arm only** |
| `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` | `ContactSection.astro:349`, `BookingForm.svelte:1115` | `:526`, `:606` |
| `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT` | `book.astro:143`, `ContactSection.astro:351`, `BookingForm.svelte:1119` | `:1151` via `slice(0, 2)` |
| `FEE_TERMS_REFUND` | terms box | `:532`, `:609` |
| `FEE_TERMS_CREDIT` | terms box | `:533`, `:611` |
| `CANCEL_LINE` | terms box, as `FEE_TERMS_REFUND[4]` (`booking-copy.ts:509`) | `:574`, `:627`, `:771`, `:804` |
| `RECEIVED_NEXT_STEPS` | `BookingConfirmation.svelte:149` | `:491`, `:595` |

**So "the terms box" is not a web surface. It is a shared surface**, and the
edit that rewrites it lands on every customer email in the same keystroke.

🔴 **Why this is a blocker and not a note.** `CONVERSION` §7 constraint 5 —
the rule decision 24 invoked to choose this axis — requires the copy edit and
its pin to be **one commit**. Under a literal reading of decision 24's table:

- BK-53 deletes `FEE_TERMS_REFUND`. `FEE_TERMS_REFUND[4]` **is** `CANCEL_LINE`,
  so that commit silently removes one of `CANCEL_LINE`'s two renders in every
  customer email.
- The pin that would catch it — *"positively pin the survivor at
  `booking-email.ts:574`/`:627`"* — is **BK-55's**, by BK-55 §4.
- Between the two commits the claim is live and unpinned. **That is precisely
  the failure decision 24 chose this axis to avoid**, reproduced inside it.

### ✅ RESOLUTION — the seam rule. Both tickets adopt it verbatim.

1. **`src/lib/booking-copy.ts` belongs to BK-53, whole.** Every constant in it,
   and **every assertion in any verify script that reads a `booking-copy.ts`
   symbol** — `verify-cutover.ts` and `verify-booking-email.ts` alike — is
   BK-53's to edit, in BK-53's commit. Constant and pin stay in one commit,
   which is the whole of constraint 5.
2. **BK-55 owns claims that ORIGINATE in `booking-email.ts` / `resend.ts`** —
   the computed `assessmentSummary`/`tierLine` line (R4-1), the `APPROVED_*`
   terms wording (decision 19), the `resend.ts` suppression (R4-3), ~~the
   `slice(0, 2)` index addressing,~~ ⛔ *(struck with rule 3 — the array is
   deleted, so there is no index addressing to own)* and the
   `verify-booking-email.ts` arms over **those**. BK-55 does **not** edit
   `booking-copy.ts`.
   ⚠️ 🔴 **AMENDED BY SEAM CLAUSE 6 (R8-6d):** where BK-55's own commit reddens
   an arm rule 1 gives BK-53, BK-55 makes the minimum edit that restores green.
   **Rule 1 assigns the DESIGN; it does not license a red commit.**
3. ⛔ **STRUCK IN PLACE 2026-09-06 (R7-6b · R8-7 🟠19 · R8-10 T8). THE ORDER
   STANDS; THIS RATIONALE IS VOID AND ITS INSTRUCTION IS WRONG.**
   ~~*The `slice(0, 2)` interaction is BK-55's… BK-53 must not land before BK-55
   has converted the index addressing to named constants — otherwise shrinking
   the array silently changes what a confirmed customer reads and no arm
   fails.*~~
   🔴 **The array is DELETED, not shrunk** (R5-10 T1), which makes
   `booking-email.ts:44` and `:1151` **compile errors — loud, not silent** — and
   **BK-55 §B-2 replaced the conversion with outright deletion, so no
   named-constants conversion is to be written by anyone.** *An implementer
   reading this rule literally would write code no ticket asks for.*
   ✅ **The order is still BK-55 → BK-53, for the opposite reason (BK-55 §E-1):**
   BK-53 cannot delete an export while `booking-email.ts` still imports it, so
   BK-55 must remove the email's dependence first.
   ⚠️ **R7-6b ordered this strike, R8-7 ordered it again, and both performed it
   in prose one section away. This is the third time of asking and the first
   time it is written here.**
4. 🔴 **The adversarial pass runs ONCE, at the TIP of `bk51-gated`, after
   BK-51 + BK-55 + BK-53 have all landed — never against BK-55's commit alone.**
   An adversarial pass against a mid-branch commit breaks code that will never
   ship in that state. Its scope is the **union** of both halves. This is what
   keeps BK-53 honestly at **Reviewed** while its email renders still get
   adversarial coverage.

⚠️ **Decision 24's stated reasons survive this rule intact:** the money and the
write path are still in one ticket (R4-1, R4-3, decision 19 are all BK-55's),
and BK-53 is still the smaller, plainer half. What changes is only *where the
constant file lives* — and it has to live somewhere whole, or constraint 5 is
violated by construction.

## R5-2 · 🔴 THE GATE CANNOT EMIT THE NUMBER THE TICKET ORDERS IT TO LOG

**Also new, also in no review, and it voids R4's own remedy.**

Revision 3 binds the implementer to *"**Log the summary line's ASSERTION
COUNT**, not just the exit code"*, and R4 sharpens it to *"pre-register the
expected count per script IN THIS TICKET before touching code, and assert
`(exit code, summary line)`"* — CLAUDE.md's BK-33 rule.

**Verified in all three scripts: there is no count to log.**

```ts
let failures = 0;
function check(condition: boolean, message: string) {
  if (!condition) { console.error(`  ✗ ${message}`); failures++; }
}
...
if (failures > 0) { console.error(`\n✗ ${failures} check${...} failed\n`); process.exit(1); }
console.log('\n✓ cutover checks passed\n');          // verify-cutover.ts:1231-1235
```

`check()` counts **only failures**. The success summary line is a bare
`✓ … checks passed` with **no number** — identical text whether 91 assertions
ran or 4 did. Same in `verify-booking-pricing.ts` (`✓ booking pricing checks
passed`) and `verify-booking-email.ts` (`✓ booking notification checks passed`).

🔴 **So the only number any of them ever prints is the `✗` count — the exact
quantity CLAUDE.md's BK-33 trap forbids scoring on.** Pre-registering an
expected assertion count against these scripts is unfalsifiable: nothing
observable can contradict it. A whole `describe`-equivalent block could be
deleted and the summary line would not change by one character.

### ✅ RESOLUTION — R5-2a, and it is three lines per script

Add a pass counter and print it. **BK-53 does this to the two scripts it owns;
BK-55 does the same to `verify-booking-email.ts`. No seam.**

```ts
let failures = 0;
let passes = 0;
function check(condition: boolean, message: string) {
  if (!condition) { console.error(`  ✗ ${message}`); failures++; return; }
  passes++;
}
...
// BOTH branches carry the number — R5-11 A9. A red run is exactly when
// "score on (exit code, summary line)" needs a count, and the original
// version of this snippet printed one only on success.
if (failures > 0) {
  console.error(`\n✗ ${passes} passed, ${failures} failed\n`);
  process.exit(1);
}
console.log(`\n✓ cutover checks passed — ${passes} passed, 0 failed\n`);
```

🔴 **A scalar count is not enough on its own (R5-11 A9).** `verify-cutover.ts`'s
sweeps are **one** `check(offenders.length === 0)` over a filtered array — the
count is **1 whether the filter read 40 files or 0**. And a scalar detects only
**net** deletion: drop an arm, add any `check(true, …)`, and count, exit code and
summary line are byte-identical. **→ Every sweep additionally asserts its
SCANNED-FILE COUNT is non-zero and within a pre-registered range.** A file-count
assertion is the only thing that distinguishes *"nothing offended"* from
*"nothing was read"*.

**Do this FIRST, as the first commit of the ticket, before any copy moves** —
the pre-registered numbers in R5-6 are meaningless until the scripts can report
against them. It is additive, it cannot redden anything, and it is the only way
the red-first evidence in this ticket is worth reading.

⚠️ **Count assertions RUN, not `check(` call sites.** Several calls sit inside
`forEach`/`for` loops — `verify-booking-pricing.ts:73-88` runs its body three
times, once per tier — so the call-site count (**41**, per C-f) is *not* the
runtime count, and the two must not be conflated in the pre-registration table.

   tell you the amount when we confirm your appointment."*

## R5-5 · THE GATE

### 5a · The coupling pin, re-derived around the noun the copy standard mandates

> **A surface that claims the assessment is free, OR releases obligation, must
> carry the travel-fee qualifier. Claim and qualifier travel together, or red.**

🔴 **R4-4 was right and revision 3's shapes stay wrong until this lands.**
R3-BL-4 mandated *"ONE canonical phrase, and the noun is **visit**"*, and §2a
then anchored every regex on **"assessment"**. Three of the approved drafts
match nothing: `ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE` (*"The visit is free whichever you pick"*),
`llms.txt.ts:54` (*"the visit itself is free"* — **and it is the surface most
likely to be quoted out of context**), and `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS[0]` (*"…what they
find. **Free.**"* — shape 1 needs `free` **before** `assessment`, and the `.` is
excluded by `[^.<>]`).

```ts
const FREE_CLAIM_SHAPES = [
  // the noun the copy standard mandates, both grammars, both orders
  /\bfree[^.<>]{0,40}?\b(?:visits?|assessments?)\b/i,
  /\b(?:visits?|assessments?)\b[^.<>]{0,40}?\b(?:is|are|'s|itself is)\s+free\b/i,
  // the standalone predicate — FEE_TERMS_ITEMS[0]'s "…what they find. Free."
  /(?:^|[.>])\s*free\.\s*(?:$|[<'"`,])/im,   // the trailing quote — R5-11 A7
  // obligation-release, decision 23 — PLURAL AND HYPHEN VARIANTS ARE THE POINT
  /\bno[\s-]+obligations?\b/i,
  /\bwithout\s+obligation\b/i,
  /\bno\s+(?:commitment|strings|cost|charge)\b/i,
  /\b(?:nothing is charged|not charged|nothing to pay|at no cost)\b/i,
  /\b(?:don'?t|do not|won'?t)\s+charge\b/i,
  /\b(?:pay nothing|costs? you nothing|complimentary|waived?)\b/i,
  /\$\s*0(?:\.00)?\b/,
];
const TRAVEL_QUALIFIER = /\btravel\s+fees?\b/i;  // \s+ and s? — R5-11 A10
```

⚠️ **`no[\s-]+obligations?` — the `+` and the `s?` are both load-bearing.**
A one-character edit (*"no obligations"*) turns a `no[\s-]obligation` pin off,
and Prettier reflow inserts a second space. **This is BK-48 verbatim, in the
newest pin in the ticket.**

**Five corrections that make it non-vacuous:**

- **A1 — scope follows where the FINAL copy lands.** §R5-4 puts claims as
  **inline literals** into `book.astro`, `ContactSection.astro` and
  `[service].astro`. **Scan: `booking-copy.ts`, every file in R5-4's table,
  `BookingForm.svelte:780-782` (R5-10 T9), and `dist/`.**
- 🔴 **A1a — `booking-copy.ts` is scanned PER CONSTANT, with a named exempt
  list. (R5-10 T3, and it is not optional.)** Scanned whole, this pin reddens
  `NO_CHARGE_LINE` — which R5-9's A3 promises to leave unchanged. **Two rows of
  this revision contradicted each other**, which is round 1's B2 in its fifth
  incarnation. The per-constant form is the pattern
  `verify-booking-email.ts:368-372` already uses and R3B-C2 already made binding.
  **The principle that decides membership: the qualifier is owed where the
  customer has not yet been told, and not where they have.** Exempt, each with
  its reason in the code: `NO_CHARGE_LINE` (confirmation email — the fee is
  already settled and disclosed), `DECLINED_LEAD`, `EXPIRED_REQUEST_LEAD`,
  `PAYMENT_EXPIRED_LEAD` (facts about a terminated booking, not offers).
  ⚠️ **Assert the exempt list is non-growing.** An exemption is how a ban dies
  quietly.
- **A2 — proximity is tested AT SOURCE, per rendered block**, not whole-file.
  Whole-file on `dist/index.html` passes with the claim in the hero and the
  qualifier in a component hundreds of lines away. `dist/` stays a **presence**
  backstop only, and its comment must say so. ⚠️ **The supporting quotation in
  revision 3 was fabricated — see C-e.** `verify-cutover.ts:440-453` does assert
  the terms constants sit in the CTA arm and **never** the form arm, and it is
  worth keeping for that reason alone; it carries no comment about having
  shipped green.
- **A3 — the `.js` arm is excluded from the coupling check** (kept for the plain
  ban): `bundles` is `dist/client/**` filtered to `.js` and `.html`
  (`verify-cutover.ts:972-977`), and chunk co-location is a Vite decision nobody
  reviews.
- **A5 — the POSITIVE TWIN.** The coupling pin is a *conditional* and passes
  vacuously: **delete the eyebrow entirely and every gate stays green.** Assert
  `dist/client/book/index.html` and `dist/client/index.html` each carry a free
  claim **and** a qualifier.
- 🆕 **A6 — the twin needs a SOURCE sibling.** `dist/` is populated by the
  sentinel build the script runs itself (`verify-cutover.ts:946`), and its own
  closing line warns the tree is a sentinel build. A twin that lives only in the
  `dist/` arm inherits every fragility A2 assigns to it. **Add the same
  present-and-qualified assertion over `src/sections/ContactSection.astro` and
  `src/pages/book.astro` at source.**

### 5b · 🔴 THE BANS — R4-5 WAS RIGHT AND NAMED THREE SITES. THERE ARE **FOUR**.

R4-5 re-scoped `:512-517`, `:1056-1071` and `:1188-1193`. **It omitted
`verify-cutover.ts:471-477`, and that is the one that fails first.**

| Site | Assertion | Scope | What the approved copy does to it |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **`:471-477`** 🆕 | `!/no obligation/i.test(prose)` over `book.astro` and `src/sections/ContactSection.astro` | **whole file** — `stripForUse(readFileSync(...))`, frontmatter included | 🔴 **RED** — off `book.astro:36-37`'s new **frontmatter** meta description, which this arm reads and `:512-517` does not. ⚠️ **R5-11 A8 corrects the reason:** decision 23 puts the terms-box sentence in **`booking-copy.ts`**, which *neither* ban reads. The finding stands; two of its three original reasons did not |
| `:512-517` | `!/free\s+assessment/i.test(template)` over the same two files | **template only** — `templateOf(raw, file)` | ⚠️ **GREEN — and this row said RED, wrongly (corrected by R5-11 A8, by execution).** `\s+` is literal whitespace and *"Free **on-site** assessment"* puts `on-site ` in the gap. **It must be re-scoped BECAUSE IT IS VACUOUS against the copy that ships**, not because it fails. The frontmatter description escapes this arm entirely — the two bans have different scopes |
| `:1055-1071` | `BANNED_SHAPES` over every `dist/client/**/*.{js,html}` | built output | 🔴 **RED** — `/\bfree[^.<>]{0,40}?assessments?\b/i` matches *"Free on-site assessment"* in `dist/client/index.html` and `dist/client/book/index.html`, **the two files A5's twin requires to carry it** |
| `:1188-1193` | `BANNED` over `dist/client/llms.txt` | built output | ⚠️ **GREEN — and that is the problem.** The draft reads *"an on-site assessment — about 30 minutes, and the visit itself is free"*; the gap between *assessment* and *is free* is far past `{0,24}`, so **neither today's ban nor a naive coupling pin sees the surface most likely to be quoted bare.** Fixed by the re-derived shapes in 5a, not by the ban |

**→ All four are re-scoped to the coupling form — claim permitted WITH
qualifier, red without — in the SAME commit as the copy.** ⚠️ **`:1188-1193`
must be re-pointed even though it is green today**; leaving it is the
negative-assertion trap in its BK-48 shape — *the pin is named for a category
and shaped for one claim*.

### 5c · R4-2 — the form prices every radio, and the gate positively requires it

**Verified.** `BookingForm.svelte:1171-1175` sits in its **own** `{#if tierQuotes}`
inside `{#each ASSESSMENT_TIERS}` (`:1154-1182`) — **not** inside the
`{#if quote}` block at `:1198-1217` that R5-4 deletes:

1169  <span class="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-2">
1170    <span class="font-semibold text-yeg-text">{ASSESSMENT_TIER_NAMES[tier]}</span>
1171    {#if tierQuotes}

1173        {formatCents(tierQuotes[tier].baseCents)} + GST
1174      </span>
1175    {/if}

🔴 `verify-cutover.ts:594-598` **positively requires it** —
`island.includes('formatCents(tierQuotes[tier].baseCents)')`, as the third
clause of a triple-AND — and revision 3's neighbouring instruction is *"KEEP
`verify-cutover.ts:600-603`"*, so an implementer reads the whole region as a
keep.

**→ Delete `:1171-1175`. Re-point `:594-598` to assert the amounts are ABSENT
while the tier LABELS remain. `:600-603` stays** (*"the island contains no price
arithmetic of its own"* — **more** valuable once no price renders, and its
opening `check(` sits inside a range revision 2 told an implementer to rewrite,
R3B-D3).

re-justify or move them back.** *A deleted assertion is an unpinned claim.*
🔴 **Restored from round 1:** `verify-cutover.ts:571-579` (pins
`QUOTE_HEADING` and `AFTER_HOURS_NOTE` **present**, both deleted here — **D1**),
`verify-booking-email.ts:742-745` (the `$0` message's `!/\$[\d,]+\.\d{2}/`,
whose justifying comment cites the very figures being removed).

⚠️ **Every `verify-booking-email.ts` arm listed above reads a `booking-copy.ts`
symbol, so all of them are BK-53's under seam rule 1.** The arms that read
`assessmentQuote`, `APPROVED_*` or `resend.ts` are BK-55's. **If an arm reads
both, it is BK-53's** — the constant is the thing being changed.

**Rules that bind the implementer:**
- ⚠️ **DELETE constants; never EMPTY them.** An emptied array makes
  `for (const line of carries)` run **zero iterations and stay green having
  asserted nothing** (R1-B3).
- ⚠️ **Pin the orphaned readers' ABSENCE.** `tsconfig.json` extends
  `astro/tsconfigs/strict`, which does **not** set `noUnusedLocals`;
  `verify-cutover.ts:558-559` records orphaned imports raising no error when a
  fieldset was deleted. Once `AFTER_HOURS_NOTE` and the quote block go, `quote`,
  `tierQuotes`, `assessmentQuote` and `formatCents` are dead in the island.
- ⇄ **Scope the payment-link ban PER CONSTANT**, the pattern
  `verify-booking-email.ts:368-372` already uses — a whole-file ban on
  `booking-copy.ts` cannot exclude `PAYMENT_CANCELLED_LEAD`, which lives in it
  (R3B-C2).
- ⇄ **§5a's `/\bEverything above\b/i` is replaced by a positive twin:** each
  non-`standard` description must match `/quoted with your job/i`. The old ban
  was shaped for a sentence being deleted, not for the claim (R3B-D5).

## R5-7 · SEQUENCING, THE DRAIN, AND THE PUSH

**Commit order on `bk51-gated`** — seam rule 3 fixes it:

1. BK-51 (already on the branch)
2. **BK-55** — including the `slice(0, 2)` → named constants conversion
3. **BK-53** — R5-2a first, then the copy + pins
4. **The adversarial pass, at the TIP, over the union** (seam rule 4)
5. One push

**On `main`, BK-53 alone is a priceless site with a live charging screen** —
`[id].astro:505-512` still pre-fills `assessmentQuote` and mints Checkout
Sessions, and the approval field shows **$399.00** at `:1776` while the Session
charges **$418.95**. **Never push this half alone.**

**Drain probe — run it, paste the output here, before the push:**

```sql
SELECT id, status, slot_start, payment_status, paid_amount_cents,
       refunded_amount_cents, payment_due_at, total_amount_cents
FROM appointments
WHERE status IN ('pending_review','approved_awaiting_payment','payment_expired')
   OR (status = 'confirmed' AND slot_start >= now() - interval '7 days')
ORDER BY status, slot_start;
```

⚠️ `approved_awaiting_payment` is the row that hurts — a live Checkout Session
and a deadline, untouched by a copy deploy, paid days later for a visit the site
now calls free. ⚠️ **The `confirmed` arm is the one R4 added and it is the
sharper one:** a `confirmed` row with a future `slot_start` is a customer who
**has already paid** for a visit about to be advertised free — `#36`,
`cs_live_`, **$628.43**. `pending_review` is the benign direction (decision 16
accepts those going free).

## R5-8 · TIER, AND THE BRIEF EVERY REVIEWER GETS

**Tier: Reviewed** — plan review + implementation review. Decision 24 drops the
adversarial pass from this half **because BK-55 carries it and seam rule 4 runs
it over the union at the branch tip.** If seam rule 4 is not adopted, **this
ticket goes back to Reviewed + adversarial** — the email renders of the
constants it edits are otherwise uncovered.

🔴 **Every reviewer of this ticket MUST be briefed with all four of these:**

1. **The target branch is `bk51-gated`, not `main`,** and its approval path
   differs: nothing here is a PR, `git push origin main` is the deploy, and this
   branch is never pushed. *(Two of four earlier reviewers reasoned from `main`
   and produced findings false on the branch. A wrong line number fails loudly;
   a wrong premise reads as a confident finding.)*
   🔴 **AND SAY WHICH `review.ts`.** *(Added 2026-09-06 — R9-10 nit 11, a
   defect in the BRIEF ITSELF, found by a reviewer who checked it.)* **This
   ticket's bare `review.ts:246` always means
   `src/pages/api/admin/appointments/review.ts` — the API route, where
   `if (travelCents !== 0) return { error: 'travel' };` lives.** There is a
   second divergent file, **`src/lib/booking-review.ts`, which is 171 lines on
   `main` and 209 on `bk51-gated` and HAS NO LINE 246.** A brief that expands
   the bare name to that file sends the reviewer to a line that does not exist.
   ⚠️ **Four consecutive agent briefings in one session made exactly that
   expansion.** *None of them tripped on it — but "a wrong premise reads as a
   confident finding" applies to the brief as much as to the review, and this
   brief was the thing warning about it.*
2. **Of the files this ticket touches, only `[id].astro` differs between the
   branches** — for everything else, `main` and `bk51-gated` are byte-identical.
   `[id].astro` citations must be read via `git show bk51-gated:`.
3. **The copy is APPROVED (decision 26).** Do not re-litigate wording on taste.
   Do challenge it on truth, on decision 25 (**no number on any web surface**),
   and on whether a pin actually matches it.
4. **The reviewer receives the ticket, the diff and the gate output — nothing
   else.** Enumerate every nit; do not count them in a header.

## R5-10 · TRACEABILITY AUDIT OF THIS REVISION — 2026-09-05, fresh agent

**The audit ran on revision 5 the day it was written, per the standing rule. It
found thirteen dropped rows and one self-contradiction, and it was right about
twelve of them.**

🔴 **R5-3's headline — *"Nothing below is dropped. Every item of revision 4 has a
row"* — WAS FALSE when written.** Six items had no row. **That is the fourth
consecutive revision to drop rows from the round it was written to answer**, and
the first to claim in its own text that it had not. The claim is struck; what
follows is the enumeration that should have been there.

⚠️ **Read the disposition, not the count.** Every row below is now carried,
struck with a reason, or moved to a named destination that has been checked to
contain it.

### 🔴 Blockers this audit reopened

**T1 · `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT` — three of its four members had no verdict. (R2-B1,
raised in round 2 and dropped by revisions 3, 4 and 5.)** Revision 3's heading
said the terms box *"replaces INTRO / ITEMS / **PAYMENT** / REFUND / CREDIT"*;
revision 5 deleted that phrase and named only `[1]`. Verified verbatim:

| Index | Text | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[0]` `:460` | *"**Those are our standard rates.** What you pay depends on the service you need and the time you pick — **the booking form shows that price** before you send your request, and **the approval email states the amount we will charge**."* | 🔴 **DELETE.** Every clause fails: *"Those"* dangles once `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS`'s figures go; *"the booking form shows that price"* is false the moment R4-2 deletes the radio prices; and *"the approval email states the amount we will charge"* is false on the free path |
| `[1]` `:461` | the weekend 1.5× sentence | **DELETE** — decision 21. Already W11 |
| `[2]` `:462` | *"Nothing is charged when you send your request. Once we approve it we email you **a secure payment link**, stating the amount and when we need it paid."* | **DELETE** — the payment-link claim this ticket opens with |
| `[3]` `:463` | *"If we do not have the payment in time, **we may release the time**, and you are welcome to book again."* | ⇄ **REPLACED, not deleted** — see T2 |

🔴 **`feeTermsPaymentFor` (`booking-email.ts:1150-1152`) hands `[0]` and `[1]` to
the CONFIRMED arm** — *the one message the free customer keeps*. Round 2 raised
this and predicted reviewers would miss it because they work from the table.
Three revisions later, they had.

⚠️ **Consequence for seam rule 3, and it is a real change:** the array is
**deleted**, not shortened. So BK-55's `slice(0, 2)` conversion is not a
refactor — **removing the export breaks `booking-email.ts`'s import**, and the
named-constants conversion must land the replacement lines, not merely re-address
the old ones. **BK-55 §4 is amended accordingly.**

**T2 · The slot-release pre-consent clause. (R3-SF12, explicitly restored by
revision 4, then dropped by revision 5 — and BK-55 cited it while implementing
the thing it rejected.)** R3-SF12's point was that putting terms in `APPROVED_*`
cannot repair a disclosure that first appears **in the email asking for money**;
the remedy is one clause in the terms box, which the customer sees *before*
consenting. Deleting `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT[3]` removes the only pre-consent
statement that a slot can be released.

**→ Terms box item 7, and it replaces `[3]` rather than dropping it:**
*"If we ever ask you to pay for something before the visit, we tell you the
amount and the date. If it is not paid by then we may release the time — and you
are welcome to book again."*

⚠️ **BK-55 §4 must stop citing R3-SF12 as satisfied by `APPROVED_*`.** The
`APPROVED_*` disclosure is decision 19's fix for **Gap 2** and remains correct;
it is not R3-SF12's fix, and R3-SF12's fix is here, on the web surface.

**T3 · The coupling pin reddens a constant this ticket promises not to touch.**
🔴 **My own R4-5, reproduced in the fix for it.** R5-5a's
`/\bno\s+(?:commitment|strings|cost|charge)\b/i` matches `NO_CHARGE_LINE`
(`booking-copy.ts:270`, *"There is no charge for this visit."*); A1 puts
`booking-copy.ts` in the scan scope; and R5-9's assumption A3 declares
`NO_CHARGE_LINE` **KEEP-UNCHANGED**. **Two rows of the same revision contradict
each other** — *the gate forbids the copy this ticket exists to ship*, round 1's
B2, in its fifth incarnation.

**→ RESOLVED, and not by weakening the regex.** The coupling pin scans
`booking-copy.ts` **PER CONSTANT**, with a named exempt list carrying a reason
each — **the pattern `verify-booking-email.ts:368-372` already uses and which
R3B-C2 already made binding for the payment-link ban.** The principle that
decides membership:

> **The qualifier is owed where the customer has not yet been told. It is not
> owed where they have.**

| Exempt | Reason |
| --- | --- |
| `NO_CHARGE_LINE` | Renders on the **confirmation** email (`booking-email.ts:445`) — *after* the office has decided the travel fee on the confirmation call (decision 8) and told the customer. A qualifier there says *"a fee may apply"* to someone who already knows whether it did |
| `DECLINED_LEAD`, `EXPIRED_REQUEST_LEAD`, `PAYMENT_EXPIRED_LEAD` | *"Nothing has been charged"* — a statement of fact about a **terminated** booking, not an offer |

⚠️ **The exempt list is itself a pin.** Adding a name to it must require a
comment, and the list must be asserted non-growing — an exemption is how a ban
dies quietly.

  pins to zero. ⚠️ **`HANDOFF-2026-09-05.md` §5 already struck this finding as
  verified false, and it came back within a day inside a validation pass whose
  own brief said to verify branch claims with `git show`.** **Fifth instance of
  the answered-question-still-open trap** — and the first where the re-raiser was
  an agent explicitly told how to check. **Do not re-open without quoting
  `:1857-1867`.**

### The header-only findings — the count was wrong too

The ticket's header says **13** findings exist only as digits. The audit's
enumeration says **15**: round-1 nits ×4, round-2 nits ×5 **and 3 of its 7 user
decisions**, round-3 nits ×4 **and R3-SF10** *(the round-3 should-fix list jumps
from 9 to 11)*, and round 3B's ninth should-fix *(C2, D3, D4, D5, E2, E3, E4, E5
is eight)*. **Corrected to 15 throughout.** They remain unrecoverable; a reviewer
who finds something "new" still cannot tell it from a re-discovery.

### 🙋 One item goes back to the user — it is the only one

**A client question from round 3, dropped by every revision since and in no
"still open" list: now the refund block is being deleted, does the client want a
stated cancellation-notice expectation?** `CANCEL_LINE` survives and tells the
customer **how** to cancel; `FEE_TERMS_REFUND` was the only place that said
**how much notice** is expected, and it is going. **This is policy, not
judgment** — CLAUDE.md sends it to the user. **Not a blocker on writing the
spec; it is a blocker on shipping the terms box**, because the box is where the
answer would live.

## R5-11 · ADVERSARIAL PRE-READ OF THIS REVISION — 2026-09-05, fresh agent

**Question asked: *"assuming this ships exactly as written, what still ships
GREEN?"*** Six blockers, eight should-fix, ten nits. **It converged
independently with the traceability audit on two of them** — `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT`
(its blocker 6 = R5-10 T1) and the `NO_CHARGE_LINE` contradiction (its blocker 3
= R5-10 T3), found by different methods within an hour of each other. That is
the second time on this ticket that two validators converged; **treat the
convergence as the finding, not the redundancy.**

**Every claim below was re-verified against the tree before being written down,
including the two that say this revision is wrong.**

### 🔴 A1 · DECISION 25 SHIPS WITH ZERO ENFORCEMENT — and the new pin makes the violation EASIER

**The single most important finding in either pass.** Decision 25 is the one
user instruction in this ticket marked **absolute**: *the $150 never appears on
any web surface.* **Nothing asserts it.**

**The break:** change terms item 2 to *"…a travel fee **of $150** may apply. We
tell you the amount when we confirm your appointment."* Or put `$150` in
`FEE_TERMS_ACK_LABEL` — **the sentence the customer legally ticks** — or in
`llms.txt.ts:54`, or in the meta description.

🔴 **The coupling pin PASSES, and passes harder.** The sentence contains a free
claim and `travel fee`, so **adding the number makes it more compliant.** All
four re-scoped bans pass — `BANNED_SHAPES` is shaped for `free…assessment`, not
for digits. `verify-booking-pricing.ts`'s surviving pin is on `tierDefaultCents`,
not on copy. And `verify-booking-email.ts:742-745`'s `!/\$[\d,]+\.\d{2}/` **does
not see `$150` even on the surface it guards** — no decimals. **BK-48, verbatim,
for the fourth time in this ticket's life.**

**→ W25: a `WEB_AMOUNT_BAN`, and it is a GATE, not a nicety.**

```ts
// Decision 25: no amount reaches a web surface. Currency-shaped, digit-shaped,
// and spelled-out — the number is the thing banned, not one of its spellings.
const WEB_AMOUNT_SHAPES = [
  /\$\s?[\d,]/,                                  // $150, $1,199, $ 150
  /\b\d[\d,]*\s*(?:dollars|CAD)\b/i,             // 150 dollars
  /\b(?:one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine)\s+hundred\b/i,
  /\b\d[\d,]*\s*(?:\+\s*GST|plus GST)\b/i,       // 150 + GST
];
```

**Scope: every file in R5-4's table, plus `dist/client/**/*.html`.**
⚠️ **Two carve-outs, each named and commented, never a pattern:** the phone
number (`SUPPORT_PHONE`) and *"about 30 minutes"* / *"3–5 business days"*.
**Anything else matching is a build failure.** ⚠️ **Assert the carve-out list is
non-growing** — same rule as R5-10 T3's exempt list, for the same reason.
**Red-first row 9 (new): add `$150` to terms item 2 and watch it go red.**

### 🔴 A2 · A6's SOURCE TWIN IS UNSATISFIABLE ON ONE FILE AND SATISFIED BY A `<meta>` TAG ON THE OTHER

**Verified: there is no literal string `travel fee` anywhere in `book.astro`,
`ContactSection.astro`, `BookingForm.svelte` or `booking-copy.ts` today** — the
only two hits in `booking-copy.ts` (`:267`, `:447`) are **inside comments.**

- **`ContactSection.astro` — A6 red on arrival.** The CTA card (`:284-357`)

  very file documents at `:328-332`.**
- **`book.astro` — passes for the wrong reason.** Its only literal `travel fee`
  after R5-4 is in the **frontmatter `description`**, so a `<meta>` tag would

**→ RESOLVED by R5-10 T11, and this is why T11 is a blocker rather than a
polish:** the eyebrow carries **its own literal qualifier** — *"Free on-site
assessment — travel fee may apply further out"* — on both surfaces. **A6 then
asserts a literal sentence in the rendered template, and the ticket states in

**The break A6 was written to catch, restated so it is testable:** delete
`ContactSection.astro:287`'s eyebrow entirely. A5's `dist/` twin still passes,
because the terms box on the same built page says *"The on-site assessment visit
is free."* **Green.** A6 with a literal source assertion is what catches it.

### 🔴 A3 · THE COUPLING PIN'S GRANULARITY OVER `booking-copy.ts` — both readings fail

A2 tests *"per rendered block"*. **`booking-copy.ts` has no rendered blocks.**
R5-10 T3 already chose **per constant**; this pass proves the other reading is
not merely worse but **permanently vacuous**, and adds the missing rule:

**The break under a whole-file reading:** delete the travel sentence from the
terms box **and** the travel clause from `FEE_TERMS_ACK_LABEL`, leaving
`FEE_TERMS_INTRO` alone. The acknowledged terms now claim a free visit with no
qualifier; `booking-copy.ts` still contains `travel fee`; `dist/index.html` and
`/book/` still contain it because INTRO renders on both. **Every arm green — and
W22 / decision 17a, the entire reason 17a folded into this ticket, is undone
with no red.**

⚠️ **N7, and it is the same hole from the other side: the scan MUST strip
comments.** `booking-copy.ts:221` (*"genuinely free assessment"*) and `:283`
(the site-wide sweep note) are comment-only free claims, and `:267`/`:447` are
comment-only *qualifiers*. Without `stripForUse` the pin is **red on arrival for
reasons unrelated to the copy**, and a qualifier in a comment satisfies a claim
in a string. **Use `stripForUse`, and say so in the pin's own comment.**

**Verified.** `verify-booking-email.ts:346-349` —
`refundOrder.indexOf(CANCEL_LINE) === refundOrder.length - CANCEL_LINE.length` —
is the **sole** assertion anywhere that `CANCEL_LINE` closes the block.
R5-5d put `:339-349` in **Class 3, "subject genuinely gone" → delete**, and

— **all email.**

**The break:** drop `CANCEL_LINE` from the rewritten terms array. The homepage
card, `/book/` and the island lose the only cancel-or-reschedule number a

🔴 **The subject is NOT gone** — R5-4 keeps `CANCEL_LINE` and calls it *"Not
optional"*. **`:339-349` is CLASS 2, re-point only.** ⚠️ **This is one of the
"three arms moved Class 1 → Class 3 with no per-arm justification" that this
revision warns about without naming. It is now named. Find the other two before
implementation** — the warning was inherited, and an inherited warning nobody
discharged is how this one survived four rounds.

### 🔴 A5 · DELETING `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT` MEMBERS MAKES AN ASSERTION PASS BY TESTING `undefined` — AND CRASHES THE OTHER ARM

**Verified at `verify-booking-email.ts:554-563`:** `mustNotCarry` holds
`FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT[2]` and `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT[3]` **by index**.

- `tsconfig.json` extends `astro/tsconfigs/strict`, which does **not** set
  `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` — so the type stays `string` and **typecheck is
  green** on an out-of-range index.
- **Text arm:** `body.includes(needle(undefined))` → `includes("undefined")` →
  `false` → negated → **the assertion passes having tested nothing.**
- **HTML arm:** `escapeHtml(undefined)` (`booking-email.ts:280` calls
  `value.replace(...)`) **throws** — a crash producing **no `✗` and no summary
  line**, which is **BK-33's exact misreading** and the reason R5-2a exists.

⚠️ **And there is a seam bug behind it.** BK-55 §4 says convert `:538-540`
**and** `:554-563`. But seam rule 1's tiebreak — *"if an arm reads both, it is
BK-53's"* — takes both back, and **R5-5d's class lists cover `:525-547` and OMIT
`:554-563`.** So under the specs as written, **nobody owns it.**
**→ `:554-563` is BK-53's, Class 1, and it is REWRITTEN — never re-indexed.**
Since W11 deletes the whole array (R5-10 T1), index addressing has no target at
all; the arm must name the replacement sentences directly.

### 🔴 A6 · SIX LIVE PINS POSITIVELY REQUIRE THE COPY THIS TICKET DELETES — five of them unclassified

**Verified, all in `verify-booking-email.ts`, all reading the joined
`FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT`:**

| Line | What it REQUIRES | Under the free model |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `:373` | *"FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT names the payment link"* | 🔴 red — the link claim is deleted |
| `:377` | one of `nothing is charged \| not charged \| no charge \| nothing to pay` | 🔴 red |
| `:384` | *"the appointment is confirmed BY the payment"* | 🔴 red — **decision 17 reverses it** |
| `:396` | `standard rates` **and** `booking form \| before you send` | 🔴 red |
| `:419` | reads the same array | 🔴 red |
| `:427` | reads the same array | 🔴 red |

R5-5d classified `:424` (`AFTER_HOURS_NOTE`) and **none of these six.**
**Per CLAUDE.md's BK-44 trap, unanticipated reds are exactly where rules get
loosened** — an implementer meeting six reds nobody predicted will reach for the
assertion, not the fixture.
**→ All six are Class 1, enumerated here, each rewritten to assert the
REPLACEMENT sentence rather than deleted.** ⚠️ **`:384` is the sharpest: it does
not merely go stale, it asserts the opposite of decision 17.**

🔴 **And a cross-contradiction nobody had noticed:** `:377` **requires**
`FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT` to contain one of `nothing is charged | not charged | no
charge | nothing to pay` — **every one of which is a member of
`FREE_CLAIM_SHAPES`.** Under R5-10 T3's per-constant coupling pin, `:377` and
the coupling pin **cannot both be satisfied** unless that constant also carries
`travel fee`. Since W11 deletes the array outright the collision dissolves, **but
the same collision returns the moment any replacement sentence uses those
words** — which the terms box's item 5 nearly does. **Checked: item 5 says
*"We never take money without telling you the amount first"*, which matches no
shape. Keep it that way, and say why.**

### Should-fix — each verified by execution, not by reading

- **A7 · `FREE_CLAIM_SHAPES[2]` matches nothing where A1 puts it.** Executed:
  `/(?:^|[.>])\s*free\.\s*(?:$|[<])/im` against the real source line
  `  'The assessment visit — … what they find. Free.',` → **no match.** The
  trailing `'` blocks both `$` and `<`. It matches the bare sentence and the
  rendered `…Free.</li>`, but A1 scans **source** and A2 demotes `dist/` to a
  presence-only backstop — so the one shape written explicitly for
  `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS[0]` is **dead code at the only scope that matters.**
  **→ `(?:$|[<'"`,])`.**
- 🔴 **A8 · R5-5b's row 2 IS WRONG, and this revision wrote it.** Executed:
  `/free\s+assessment/i` does **NOT** match *"Free on-site assessment"* — `\s+`
  is literal whitespace and `on-site ` sits in the gap. **`:512-517` is GREEN on
  the approved eyebrow**, not red. What actually reddens is the `dist/` sweep at
  `:1055-1071`, whose `\bfree[^.<>]{0,40}?assessments?\b` **does** match — which
  is precisely why the source ban *looked* covered. **If `:512-517` is left alone
  on the strength of a red that never happens, the source-level ban on the two
  booking surfaces is permanently vacuous against the copy that ships.** It must
  be re-scoped **because it is vacuous**, not because it fails.
  ⚠️ **Same row, second error:** decision 23 does not put *"no obligation"* "on
  exactly these two files" — it puts it in **`booking-copy.ts`**, which neither
  `:471-477` nor `:512-517` reads. `:471-477` **does** go red, but only off
  `book.astro`'s **frontmatter** meta description. *The finding stands; two of
  its three reasons were wrong.*
- **A9 · The pre-registered count is not falsifiable against substitution or a
  collapsed scan set.** (a) A single scalar detects **net** deletion only: drop
  the `[service].astro` arm, add any `check(true, …)`, and count, exit code and
  summary line are byte-identical. (b) `verify-cutover.ts`'s sweeps are **one**
  `check(offenders.length === 0)` over a filtered array — **the count is 1
  whether the filter read 40 files or 0.** If the coupling arm's file list
  resolves to nothing (a wrong `dist/client/<slug>/` path, a glob that drops
  `.html`), `offenders` is `[]`, the check passes, and `passes` does not move.
  (c) **R5-2a prints the number on the SUCCESS line only** — on a red run the
  summary line carries no count, and a red run is exactly when R5-6's *"score on
  (exit code, summary line)"* needs it.
  **→ R5-2a is amended: print `${passes} passed, ${failures} failed` on BOTH
  branches, and every sweep additionally asserts its SCANNED-FILE COUNT is
  non-zero and within a pre-registered range.** A file-count assertion is the
  only thing that distinguishes *"nothing offended"* from *"nothing was read"*.
- **A10 · `TRAVEL_QUALIFIER` has the reflow hazard this revision flags one line
  above it.** R5-5a warns that `no[\s-]+obligations?`'s `+` is load-bearing
  against Prettier, then defines `/\btravel fee\b/i` with a **literal space**.
  `[service].astro:79`'s host sentence already wraps mid-sentence in the real
  file. **Break: reflow so `travel` ends a line — the customer still reads
  "travel fee", the pin goes blind.** This is the **silent** direction.
  **→ `/\btravel\s+fees?\b/i`.**
- **A11 · Deleting `FEE_TERMS_CREDIT` silently voids `verify-cutover.ts:584-588`.**
  Verified: `outroAt = island.indexOf('FEE_TERMS_CREDIT')` becomes `-1`, so in
  `check(tierAt > 0 && tierAt > outroAt && ackAt > tierAt)` the middle clause is
  **vacuously true**. **Break: move the tier picker above the whole terms box.
  Green.** R5-5d classifies `:536-545` and `:571-579`, **not `:584-588`**.
  ⚠️ **And the trap has a second jaw:** `:546-549`'s own `check(outroAt > 0 && …)`
  **does** redden, so the implementer will be standing at that line — and the
  edit that makes it green is deleting it, **leaving `:584-588` broken and
  silent.** → Both are Class 2; re-anchor `outroAt` on the surviving closing
  constant and say which one in the ticket.
- **A12 · W10's `[id].astro` deletions have no pin and no red-first row.**
  `verify-cutover.ts` does not scan `src/pages/admin/**`, and admin pages never
  reach `dist/client`. **Break: don't delete them. Green.** → Either add an
  admin-source arm or record in terms that W10 is **unpinned and verified by
  eye** — an unpinned deletion recorded as such is honest; one that looks
  covered is not.
- **A13 · `booking-payment.ts:288` is a free claim on a MONEY artifact, in the
  crack between both tickets.** `line('Travel', 'Round trip beyond the free
  radius', …)` is a Stripe `price_data` description — **it reaches a real
  Checkout Session and a real receipt** on the hand-charged path BK-51
  preserves. BK-53 owns web; BK-55 owns `booking-email.ts`/`resend.ts`;
  `booking-payment.ts` is in neither, never reaches `dist/`, and no coupling
  scope includes it. **This is BK-48's shape exactly — a claim on an artifact

  via `ROADMAP:881-890`; this upgrades its reasoning from tidiness to a live

- **A14 · Class 1's replacement for `verify-booking-email.ts:247-254` is
  unspecified, and the obvious one is BK-48-shaped.** The arms are
  `figures.includes('399')`, `('699')`, `('1,199')`, `('GST')`. *"Rewrite to
  assert the replacement"* names no replacement, and the natural
  `!figures.includes('399')` is **a pin shaped for three specific numbers** —
  `$150`, `$385` or a re-added `$399.00` all pass at least one spelling.
  **→ The category pin: `!/\$\s?[\d,]/.test(figures)` — which is also A1's

## R5-12 · PLAN REVIEW OF REVISION 5 — 2026-09-05, fresh agent. **NOT APPROVED.**

**8 blockers · 13 should-fix · 10 nits.** Briefed per R5-8; every regex executed
rather than read; `[id].astro` read via `git show bk51-gated:`.

🔴 **THE META-FINDING, and it is the one that matters:**

> *"R5-10 and R5-11's findings were folded into the W-table and the red-first
> table, but largely not into **R5-4 (the copy)** or **R5-5 (the gate)** — the
> two sections an implementer actually builds from."*

**That is 'resolved only where it was raised' — the exact failure this ticket has
now repeated six times, committed by the fold-in that was supposed to end it.**
Revision 6 moves every resolution into the spec body, and **re-runs the
traceability audit against R5-4 and R5-5 specifically, not against the W-table.**

### The eight blockers

- **BL-1 · Deleting `FEE_TERMS_REFUND`/`_CREDIT`/`_PAYMENT` breaks
  `booking-email.ts`, and no W-row and neither ticket owns the repair.**
  `:522-533` (HTML) and `:604-611` (text) render all three. Revision 5 has **no
  row that edits those renders**, and BK-55 §0.1 forbids itself from touching
  `booking-copy.ts`. **So BK-53's commit does not typecheck.**
  🔴 **And seam rule 3's ordering makes it unfixable in place:** BK-55 lands
  first and is told to "land the replacement lines", but the replacement is
  BK-53's new terms constant, **which does not exist yet at BK-55's commit.**
  → **Re-derive the order. The render sites are BK-53's** (seam rule 1's own
  tiebreak already points there), and the ordering claim is wrong in all three
  places it appears — R5-1.3, R5-7, BK-55 §0.3/§6.
- **BL-2 · The per-constant coupling pin is RED ON ARRIVAL on approved copy.**
  Executed: `ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE` (*"The visit is free whichever you pick"*)
  hits shape 2, `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS[0]` (*"…what they find. Free."*) hits shape 3 —
  **neither carries `travel fee`, and neither can join A1a's exempt list under
  A1a's own principle**, since both render on the booking form *before* any
  confirmation call. **R5-5a deliberately re-derived those two shapes to match
  those two drafts.** Round 1's B2, sixth incarnation, authored by the fix for
  the fifth.
- **BL-3 · R5-4 still never names the constant holding the terms lines.**
  A17/N3 raised it and nothing landed. `verify-cutover.ts:350-357`'s
  `TERMS_CONSTANTS` is a hardcoded list of six **names** and `checkTermsOrder`
  asserts their render order on three surfaces; R5-5d says "Class 2, re-point
  only" — **re-point to what?** ⚠️ **This decides BL-2's outcome:** one array
  containing both the claim and the qualifier scans green; the claim and the
  qualifier in different constants scans red. **The implementer currently picks,
  and the gate's colour follows the pick.**
- **BL-4 · T5's resolution did not land in R5-4.** The copy table still reads
  `PAYMENT_CANCELLED_* — UNCHANGED` while T5 and W20 say it is fixed and supply
  the draft. **R5-4 is the section headed THE COPY.**
- **BL-5 · `WEB_AMOUNT_BAN` is red on arrival as scoped, and its carve-outs are
  vacuous.** `insurance-claims.astro:65` (*"commonly **$500 to $2,500** in
  Alberta"* — correct, unrelated copy) reaches `dist/client/`, which
  `verify-cutover.ts:945`'s own build always populates. `ContactSection.astro:65`
  holds `$699` **inside a comment** and W25 never requires `stripForUse`. And
  the three named carve-outs **match none of the four shapes** — a non-growing
  list protecting nothing while the two real offenders are absent.
- **BL-6 · The comment-stripping requirement (A3/N7) never reached R5-5a.**
  Executed over today's `booking-copy.ts`: **12 matches, 8 of them in comments.**
  Red on arrival for reasons unrelated to any customer-visible claim — and in
  the other direction `:447`'s comment-only *"travel-fee field"* would satisfy a
  claim in a string.
- **BL-7 · R5-5d orders deletion of the only pin its own red-first row 6 depends
  on.** `verify-booking-pricing.ts:68-71`'s length zip contains **no figure**,
  stays true under the new copy, and is **the only assertion in the repo that
  reddens when `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` is emptied** — `TERMS_CONSTANTS` tests the
  constant *name*, and `for (const line of carries)` runs zero iterations, which
  is R1-B3 verbatim. **The spec answers its own red-first row with "delete it".**

- **BL-8 · Red-first row 2 cannot go red.** The `s?` makes the pin **match** the

# 🟩 REVISION 4 — 2026-09-05. Revision 3's deltas. **READ THIS BEFORE REVISION 3.**

**Revision 3 went to two more fresh agents — a traceability audit and an
adversarial pre-read. They converged independently on the same three blockers.**

⚠️ **The audit's verdict, and it is fair: *"the same failure mode fired again,
harder, in the revision written to answer it."*** Revision 3 restored three
vanished rows and then **silently dropped 21 items of its own**, including two
round-1 blockers. **15 findings now exist only as digits in four review
headers** and are unrecoverable from this document. Recorded as fact, not
excuse: a revision is not a rewrite, and "supersedes every table above it" is
how rows disappear.

## 🔴 THE THREE THAT BLOCK APPROVAL

### R4-1 · The request email prices the assessment, and the GATE DEMANDS IT · restores R1-A2

**Verified on `bk51-gated`.** `booking-email.ts:1159-1168`:

```ts
const weekend = quote.afterHours ? ' — weekend rate, 1.5x' : '';
return `${name} — ${formatCents(quote.baseCents)} + GST (${formatCents(quote.totalCents)} total)${weekend}`;
```

`:411` forces `settled = null` on a request, so `tierLine` (`:456-462`) takes the
`assessmentSummary` branch for **every request email — the first message every
web customer receives.** It renders *"You chose: Assessment + written report —
$699.00 + GST ($733.95 total) — weekend rate, 1.5x"* **in the same email as
"The on-site assessment visit is free."**

⚠️ **It is computed from `assessmentQuote`, not from any constant** — decision 16
keeps `booking-pricing.ts` intact — so **no deletion in revision 3 §1 touches it,
and no pin in §2 sees it** (email bodies are in neither the scan scope nor
`dist/`). It defeats **decision 17 and decision 21 simultaneously**, on the
highest-volume surface in the ticket.

🔴 **And the fixtures REQUIRE the defect.** `verify-booking-email.ts:2013-2045`,
classified nowhere:
`check(body.includes('$399.00'))`, `check(body.includes('$418.95'))`,
`check(mouldBody.includes('$577.50'))`, `check(mouldBody.includes('$606.38'))`,
`check(mouldBody.includes('weekend rate'))` — and **`mouldBody` is the
CUSTOMER's message.** Per CLAUDE.md's BK-44 trap: establish each arm relied on
the old behaviour, **make the fixture honest, do not loosen the rule.**

**→ `assessmentSummary`/`tierLine` become a first-class §1 surface.** Under
decisions 17/20/21 the request email should name the tier and **no figure**:
`return name;`.

### R4-2 · The public form keeps a price beside every radio, and the GATE DEMANDS THAT TOO · restores R1-A1

**Verified.** `BookingForm.svelte:1169-1174` sits in its **own** `{#if tierQuotes}`
inside the `{#each ASSESSMENT_TIERS}` — **not** inside the `{#if quote}` block
revision 3 deletes. Revision 3 names it **nowhere**, and its only trace is a
subordinate clause in §2b asserting those readers become dead — **which is false
unless the span also goes.**

🔴 `verify-cutover.ts:594-598` **positively requires it**:
`island.includes('formatCents(tierQuotes[tier].baseCents)')` — and revision 3's
neighbouring instruction is *"KEEP `verify-cutover.ts:600-603`"*, so an
implementer reads the whole region as a keep.

**→ Delete the span. Re-point `:594-598` to assert the amounts are ABSENT while
the tier labels remain. `:600-603` stays.**

### R4-3 · ⚖️ R2-B2 IS NOW DECIDED — option (b), plus a new ticket for (a)

Revision 3 contained **zero occurrences** of `resend.ts`, `terms_acked_at`,
`terms_version` or `booking-commit.ts`, while the ticket header still promised
*"the `resend.ts` / terms-snapshot fix below"* and decision 19's withdrawal
recorded that shipping without it makes the defect **strictly worse**.

**DECIDED — (b), scoped to this ticket:** `resend.ts`'s confirmed-message build
**suppresses the terms block for any row whose `terms_acked_at` predates the
deploy**, and renders in its place a pointer: *"The terms you accepted are in
your original confirmation email."* **Honest, and strictly better than the two
alternatives** — sending today's free-mode terms beside a real $628.43 charge is
false; sending nothing at all contradicts `booking-email.ts:501-511`.

**(a) — the real fix — gets its own ticket: 🆕 BK-54, terms snapshot at insert.**
R2's lane C called it *"the single highest-value item"* under decision 16,
because it is **the one structure that survives an arbitrary number of pricing
flips.** It is an **insert-path** change and does not belong smuggled into a
copy ticket. **BK-54 is not a gate on BK-53.**

## 🔴 THE GATE STILL CANNOT GO GREEN — three self-contradictions

### R4-4 · `FREE_CLAIM_SHAPES` does not match revision 3's OWN COPY

R3-BL-4 mandated *"ONE canonical phrase, and the noun is **visit**"* — and §2a
then anchored all four regexes on **"assessment"**. Three of §1's own drafts
match **nothing**:

| §1 draft | Why every shape misses |
| --- | --- |
| `ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE` — *"**The visit is free** whichever you pick"* | no *"assessment"* |
| `llms.txt.ts:54` — *"the **visit itself is free**"* | no *"assessment"* — **and this is the surface most likely to be quoted out of context** |
| `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS[0]` — *"…what they find. **Free.**"* | shape 1 needs `free` before `assessment`; the `.` is excluded by `[^.<>]` |

**→ Re-derive the shapes around the noun the copy standard mandates**, and add
the grammars the adversary demonstrated: `no obligations` **(plural — a
one-character edit turns the pin off; BK-48 verbatim, in the newest pin in the
ticket)**, `without obligation`, `no commitment`, `no strings`, `no cost`,
`don't charge`, `pay nothing`, `costs you nothing`, `complimentary`, `waive`,
`$0`, and `[\s-]` → `[\s-]+` (Prettier reflow inserts a second space).

### R4-5 · `BANNED_SHAPES` and the positive twin are MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE

`verify-cutover.ts:1055-1069` fails the build if **any** built file claims a free
assessment. §2a's A5 twin **requires** two built pages to carry exactly that.
**One assertion demands the string; the other fails the build for it.**
R1-B2 named four bans; revision 3 gave a disposition to **one**.
**→ `:512-517`, `:1056-1071` and `:1188-1193` are re-scoped to the coupling
form — claim permitted WITH qualifier, red without — in the same commit.**

### R4-6 · Decision 23 constraint 2 and §6 give opposite answers

Constraint 2 binds the pin to govern `[service].astro:79`; §6 defers those four
pages; §2a's scope excludes them. **Verified:** `:79` reads *"About 30 minutes,
no obligation."* with no qualifier, and it matches the release shape. So the
`dist/` arm goes **red on day one on four pages the ticket refuses to edit** —
and the implementer's only green paths both silently discharge a constraint.
**→ RESOLVED: add the travel-fee clause to those four pages' CTA blocks — one
sentence each, no other change.** That is not the deferred question (whether
they carry the *free* claim, which still waits on the ad URLs); it is the
minimum that makes an existing live phrase honest.

## 2 · The gate

### 2a · The coupling pin — ⇄ RE-SCOPED (R3B-A1…A5)

> **A surface that claims the assessment is free, OR releases obligation, must
> carry the travel-fee qualifier. Claim and qualifier travel together, or red.**

```ts
const FREE_CLAIM_SHAPES = [
  /\bfree[^.<>]{0,40}?assessments?\b/i,
  /\bassessments?\b[^.<>]{0,24}?\b(?:is|are|'s)\s+free\b/i,
  /\b(?:no charge|nothing is charged|not charged|nothing to pay|at no cost)\b/i,  // A4
  /\bno[\s-]obligation\b/i,                                                        // decision 23
];
const TRAVEL_QUALIFIER = /\btravel fee\b/i;
```

⚠️ **Four corrections that make it non-vacuous:**

- **A1 — scope follows where the FINAL copy lands, not where today's claims
  live.** §1 puts the claim as **inline literals** into `book.astro` and
  `ContactSection.astro`. **Scan: `booking-copy.ts`, every file in §1's table,
  and `dist/`.** *(Scanning only `booking-copy.ts` + `dist/` was the previous
  reviewer's criticism reproduced in mirror image.)*
- **A2 — proximity is tested AT SOURCE, per rendered block**, not whole-file.
  Whole-file on `dist/index.html` passes with the claim in the hero and the
  qualifier in a different component hundreds of lines away — **and the enabling
  edit (moving the terms box into the `showForm` arm) is one
  `verify-cutover.ts:440-453` already exists to catch, and which its own comment
  records SHIPPED GREEN ONCE.** `dist/` stays as a **presence** backstop only,
  and its comment must say so.
- **A3 — the `.js` arm is excluded from the coupling check** (kept for the plain
  ban): chunk co-location is a Vite decision nobody reviews.
- **A5 — add the POSITIVE TWIN.** The coupling pin is a *conditional* and passes
  vacuously; **delete the eyebrow entirely and every gate stays green.** Assert
  `dist/client/book/index.html` and `dist/client/index.html` each carry a free
  claim **and** a qualifier.

### 2b · The reddened assertions — ⇄ RECLASSIFIED (R3B-B)

🔴 **`verify-booking-pricing.ts:69-88` is NOT deleted as a block. (R3B-B2.)**
The same `forEach` holds
`formatCents(tierDefaultCents(tier)) === '${expected[tier]}.00'` — **verified as
the ONLY absolute-value pin on `tierDefaultCents` in the repo** (`:107`, `:119`,
`:126` are all relative). Deleting the block lets `TIER_PRICE_CENTS` drift
`39900 → 3990` **fully green**, contradicting this ticket's own promise to keep
the engine pinned — and it is a pin bought with a recorded defect
(`"$399.90".startsWith("$399")`). **Keep `expected` and the `forEach`; delete
only the two `copy.includes(...)` checks, the length zip, and the import.
Assertion count 42 → 35 — log the drop.**

⇄ **Class 2 (structure, re-point only) is SIX arms, not two:**
`verify-cutover.ts:347-357`, `:395-416`, **`:440-453`** (asserts the terms sit in
the CTA arm and **never** the form arm — the invariant holds verbatim; treating
it as a copy pin invites deleting it), `:571-579`, `verify-booking-email.ts:525-547`,
`:590-600`.
⇄ **Class 3 (subject genuinely gone):** `verify-booking-email.ts:339-349`,
`:374`, `:424`, `verify-cutover.ts:605-619`.
🔴 **Three arms named in round 1 that VANISHED from revision 2 — restored:**
`verify-cutover.ts:571-579` (`QUOTE_HEADING` pinned present, only render inside
the deleted block — **D1**), `verify-booking-email.ts:742-745` (the `$0`
message's `!/\$[\d,]+\.\d{2}/`, whose justifying comment cites the very
figures being removed), and **R1-D5** (`mustNotCarry` built from three deleted
symbols; `booking-email.ts:1150`'s `slice(0,2)` index addressing → convert to
named constants).

**Rules that bind the implementer:**
- ⚠️ **DELETE constants; never EMPTY them.** An emptied array makes
  `for (const line of carries)` run **zero iterations and stay green having
  asserted nothing** (R1-B3).
- ⚠️ **Log the summary line's ASSERTION COUNT**, not just the exit code.
- ⚠️ **Pin the orphaned readers' ABSENCE.** `tsconfig.json` extends
  `astro/tsconfigs/strict`, which does **not** set `noUnusedLocals`;
  `verify-cutover.ts:558-559` records orphaned imports raising no error when a
  fieldset was deleted. Once `AFTER_HOURS_NOTE` and the quote block go,
  `quote`, `tierQuotes`, `assessmentQuote` and `formatCents` are dead in the
  island.
- ✅ **KEEP `verify-cutover.ts:600-603`** (*"the island contains no price
  arithmetic of its own"*) — **more** valuable once no price renders. Its
  opening `check(` sits inside the range revision 2 told an implementer to
  rewrite (R3B-D3).
- ⇄ **Scope the payment-link ban PER CONSTANT**, the pattern
  `verify-booking-email.ts:368-372` already uses — a whole-file ban on
  `booking-copy.ts` cannot exclude `PAYMENT_CANCELLED_LEAD`, which lives in it
  (R3B-C2).
- ⇄ **§5a's `/\bEverything above\b/i` is replaced by a positive twin:** each
  non-`standard` description must match `/quoted with your job/i`. The old ban
  was shaped for a sentence being deleted, not for the claim (R3B-D5).

## 3 · ⇄ `/book/payment-cancelled/` and `/book/confirmed/` — KEEP, and the reasoning is corrected

Revision 2 merged them into one sentence **true of one and false of the other.**

- **`/book/confirmed/` IS gated** — `planConfirmationRender`
  (`booking-confirmation.ts:154-173`) redirects to `/book/` without a session id.
- 🔴 **`/book/payment-cancelled/` is FULLY STATIC** — no island, no session read,
  no redirect. Its own header says *"every word of it is true for anyone who
  arrives."* **Anyone who types or shares the URL sees "the payment link in your
  approval email."**

**Both KEPT** (deleting either 404s a live `cancel_url`), both `noindex` and
sitemap-excluded, nothing links to them. **Recorded, not fixed:**
`/book/confirmed/`'s gate is a shape check the module itself calls *"A BAR, NOT
A PROOF"* — `?session_id=cs_test_aaaaaaaa` renders `PAID_*` and fires the Ads
conversion for anyone. Unchanged by BK-53.

## 4 · Sequencing and the drain

**Built on `bk51-gated`; BK-51 + BK-53 ship in ONE push.** On `main`,
`[id].astro:505-512` still pre-fills `assessmentQuote` and mints Checkout
Sessions — BK-53 alone is a priceless site with a live charging screen.

**Drain probe — run it, paste the output here, before the push:**

```sql
SELECT id, status, payment_due_at, total_amount_cents
FROM appointments
WHERE status IN ('pending_review','approved_awaiting_payment','payment_expired')
ORDER BY status, slot_start;
```

⚠️ `approved_awaiting_payment` is the row that hurts — it holds a live Checkout
Session and a deadline, a copy deploy touches none of them, and the customer
pays days later for a visit the site now calls free. `pending_review` is the
**benign** direction (decision 16 accepts those going free).

## 5 · Tier

**Reviewed + FULL adversarial pass.** The copy-only claim is struck: it changes
what the appointment insert's consent attests to, moves money claims on a live
send path, removes the live price display from the public form, and its main
artifact **is** the pin set — the highest-value adversarial target this project
has (BK-51: 11 breaks, 11 green, in a suite nobody was rewriting).

## 6 · Still open

**One, and it is not blocking:** whether the four `[service].astro` pages carry
the free claim — **blocked on reading the ad landing pages** (GA4 `invalid_grant`
+ extension not connected). **Default until answered: they are NOT in this
ticket.** They carry no price claim, so nothing on them becomes false; the
exposure is an omission and adding them later is one commit.
