# ✅ DECISIONS 20–22 — user, 2026-09-05 (second round). THESE CHANGE THE SCOPE.

## Decision 20 — NOTHING IS HANDED TO THE CUSTOMER IN WRITING AT THE ASSESSMENT

**User, verbatim:** *"client assesses the site/damage, **nothing is handed over
to the customer in writing.** if customer wants to proceed with the client, then
**the quote and the other things follow**."* And: *"of course quote will be
given and the customer can decide which will include reports and different
service tiers."*

| Stage | What the customer gets |
| --- | --- |
| **The free visit** | The technician assesses. **No written document. Nothing.** |
| **After, if they want to proceed** | **A quote**, which is where reports and the service tiers appear as options |
| **The work** | Quoted, as before |

⚠️ **THIS RETIRES A DELIVERABLE WE INVENTED, NOT ONE THE CLIENT SOLD.** The
client's settled tier table (`BK-27.md:213-217`, 2026-08-14) read
**`| Assessment | $399 + GST | The on-site assessment. No documents. |`** — and
BK-36 added *"and a written scope of the damage"* on 2026-08-19 without listing
it among its five recorded departures from the approved draft. **Decision 20
restores the client's original position.** `booking-copy.ts:402` and `:581` are
now describing something that does not happen.

## 🔴 Decision 20's REAL cost: a "written scope" inventory across FIVE surfaces, two outside this ticket

**The seam is clean and must be applied claim-by-claim, not file-by-file:**

| Verdict | Shape | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ✅ **STAYS TRUE** | *"a written scope **before work starts**"* | Under decision 20 the quote precedes the work, so a scope arriving with it is honest |
| 🔴 **NOW FALSE** | *"a written scope **at the assessment**"* | Nothing is handed over at the visit |

**Still true — do NOT sweep these:** `services.ts:262` (*"before any work
starts"*), `:226` (*"with timelines before work starts"*), `:106`, `:346`,
`TestimonialsSection.astro:18-19` (*"A written scope before work starts"*),
`about.astro:195` (*"up front"*), `llms.txt.ts:38`,
`insurance-claims.astro:30`/`:270` (both scoped to *"every **job**"*).

**🔴 NOW FALSE — five surfaces:**

| Surface | The claim |
| --- | --- |
| `booking-copy.ts:402` `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS[0]` | *"the on-site assessment **and a written scope of the damage**"* |
| `booking-copy.ts:581` `ASSESSMENT_TIER_DESCRIPTIONS.standard` | *"**writes up the scope of the damage**"* |
| 🔴 **`insurance-claims.astro:70`** | *"**At the assessment we give you a written scope with real costs**"* — and *"real costs"* is a repair estimate, the **$699** tier |
| 🔴 **`services.ts:389`** (sewage FAQ) | *"**The assessment gives you a written scope**"* |
| 🔴 **`services.ts:291`** (mould FAQ) | *"we start with **an assessment and a written scope**, so you know the cost before any work begins"* |

⚠️ **The last three are on pages BK-53 does not currently touch, and none is

`/insurance-claims/` and inside `services.ts` FAQ answers. **`verify-cutover.ts`'s
five-file `CLAIM_SURFACES` sees none of them.** This is CLAUDE.md's
copy-inventory trap at its purest, and it is the **third instance found in this
ticket alone.**

⚠️ **`insurance-claims.astro:70` was recorded this morning as a moderate
out-of-scope defect on the grounds that it gave away the $699 deliverable.
Decision 20 escalates it: it is now a false statement about what the visit
produces**, on a live page, in the claim-filing context where a customer is most
likely to rely on it. **It needs an owner and it does not wait for BK-53.**

## Decision 21 — THE WEEKEND SURCHARGE IS DEAD. Verbatim, at last.

**User, twice, 2026-09-05:** *"we decided to get rid of the weekend surcharge"*
and *"no weekend surcharge (clarified before, asked again)."*

⚠️ **This supplies what decision 7 never had.** Decision 7 was client-*attributed*
but carried **no verbatim quote**, unlike decisions 11, 12, 13, 15 and 17 — and

bookings, not only emergencies" both being OUR inference**, written into the

## Decision 22 — THE TRAVEL-FEE PROMISE IS SOFTENED

**User, verbatim:** *"no need to promise, if a travel fee is applied, customer
is informed based on their location when client confirms the booking."*

⇄ **The drafted *"never after the visit"* is STRUCK.** It was a commitment about
the client's own conduct that no code enforces. Replacement:

> *"If your address is outside our service area, a travel fee may apply. We tell
> you the amount when we confirm your appointment."*

**Also settled: R3-BL-5 and R3-CQ-2 close.** *"May we print 'never after the
visit'"* — **no.**

## Decision 23 — *"NO OBLIGATION"* GOES BACK ON

**User, 2026-09-05:** *"yes put no obligation back."*

**It is true under decisions 17 and 20:** the visit is free, **nothing is handed
over**, and a customer who declines owes nothing and receives nothing. The one
recorded objection — `PREPAY-PLAN:516-519`, *"a decline fee wearing a travel
fee's name"* — **depended on the travel fee being WAIVED if the customer
proceeds.** Decision 17 charges it either way, so **that objection does not
survive and is marked superseded rather than left standing.**

**Two binding constraints on how it ships:**

1. ⚠️ **It must NEVER sit next to a bare "free" without the travel-fee
   qualifier.** *"Free, no obligation"* alone reads as *"this cannot cost me
   anything under any circumstance"* — false for an out-of-zone customer, and
   the pre-prepay claim's shape re-armed in the opposite direction.
2. ⇄ **`verify-cutover.ts:470-475` is REPLACED, not merely struck.** §1
   previously said *"this ticket removes the prohibition and asserts nothing in
   its place"* — **that is now wrong**, and it would leave the phrase ungoverned
   on the one surface where it is already live (`[service].astro:79`).
   **Extend the coupling pin to treat an obligation-release exactly like a free
   claim: release + qualifier travel together, or red.**

**Draft:** *"About 30 minutes, no obligation. A travel fee may apply outside our
service area; we tell you when we confirm."*

⚠️ **This also discharges W20, which is recorded as BACKWARDS** — W20 exists to
*strike* the phrase, and under decisions 17/20/23 it is true and is the best
claim available. **BK-53 absorbs W20's surface; W20 is struck**, the same move
decision 17a made for W22. **W21 is NOT struck — it changes shape:** the ad says
*"free, no-obligation **quote**"*, and under decision 20 the **quote** is not the
free thing, the **visit** is. W21 becomes *fix the noun*, still after W3.

## Still open after this round

✅ **NONE. The last open item closed 2026-09-05 — MEASURED, not inferred.**

**GA4 (property 543677881), paid sessions 2026-07-01→09-05: the HOMEPAGE takes
1,097 of ~1,204 — about 92%. The four `[service].astro` pages take 23 between
them (1.9%), and `/fire-smoke-damage` has received ZERO paid clicks.**

they are the ad entry points was one unsourced sentence in `CONVERSION` §4; it
is now measured false, and it had shaped W20's scope and was raised three times
in this ticket's reviews.

✅ **The real ad entry point is already in scope.** `index.astro:66` renders
`<ContactSection showForm={false} />` — the CTA arm `verify-cutover.ts:440-453`
pins the terms box into — so the free claim and its travel-fee qualifier land on
the page 92% of paid traffic actually sees. `HeroSection.astro` carries no
price, free or obligation claim (checked). **A5's positive twin over
`dist/client/index.html` was therefore right, and is now right for a measured
reason rather than a guessed one.**

⚠️ **R4-6 still stands, and is now cheap.** `[service].astro:79`'s
*"no obligation"* must still gain the travel-fee clause — one sentence on each
of four pages — or decision 23's coupling pin goes red on them at build time.
**That is a gate obligation, not a traffic-driven one**, and at 1.9% of paid
sessions it is the minimum honest fix rather than a copy project.

🚧 **ATTEMPTED 2026-09-05 AND BLOCKED. It needs the user.** Both automated
routes are dead:

| Route | Result |
| --- | --- |
| GA4 MCP | `503 … invalid_grant: Bad Request` — this is **W9**, already on the 🙋 index |
| Chrome extension | *"Browser extension is not connected"* |
| Playwright | **Not a substitute** — it launches a clean browser with no Google session |

⚠️ **GA4 is the better instrument than the asset-group final URL**, and the
distinction matters here: a final URL says where traffic was *configured* to go;
**GA4's landing-page report says where it actually WENT** — and §4 notes the
campaign is **Performance Max**, whose final-URL expansion sends traffic to
pages no asset group names. So re-authing GA4 (W9) answers this question
*better* than the originally planned check, **and simultaneously unblocks W5/W6**.

**Manual path if the user prefers:** Google Ads → the *Water Damage Restoration*
campaign → **Landing pages** — it lists the real URLs with clicks against each.
**Read-only; do not edit any asset** (constraint 3: W3 precedes W21, because
editing an asset can clear its policy state).

**Until this is answered, BK-53 does NOT include the four service pages.** They
carry no price claim, so nothing on them becomes false — the exposure is an
omission, not a contradiction, and it is reversible in a later commit.

# 📝 PLAN REVIEW ROUND 3 — copy & truthfulness lane, 2026-09-05

**6 blockers, 12 should-fix, 4 nits, 7 client questions.** *"The shape is right
and §5a's implicature finding is the best thing in the ticket. But six drafted
sentences are false or unbacked on the charged path, three recorded client
requirements are silently dropped, and the ack label is not adequate consent."*

## ⚠️ FIRST — TWO FINDINGS CORRECTED. The reviewer read `main`'s semantics onto `bk51-gated`.

**R3-BL-1 and R3-BL-5 both rest on:** *"Enter a travel fee at approval and
`totalCents > 0`, so the row goes down the paid branch — a payment link, a
deadline, slot release, and no calendar invite."*

**That is false on the branch this ticket is built on.** Verified:

```
bk51-gated:review.ts:246   if (travelCents !== 0) return { error: 'travel' };
bk51-gated:review.ts:212   **A travel fee cannot be approved here at all.**
```

**BK-51 refuses a non-zero travel fee outright.** Decision 8 puts it entirely
outside the software, and BK-51 *enforces* that in code. **A travel fee can
never mint a Checkout Session, a deadline, or a slot release.** It is collected
on the phone / on site, off-system.

**This is the branch-drift hazard BK-52 was annotated for, arriving from the
opposite direction** — not a stale line number, but stale *semantics*: a
reviewer reasoning about `main`'s behaviour while reviewing a plan targeting
`bk51-gated`. **Recorded as a standing hazard for every remaining BK-53 review:
brief reviewers that the target branch is `bk51-gated` and its approval route
differs from `main`'s.**

**What survives the correction:**
- **R3-BL-1 survives in weaker form.** The paid branch is still reachable — by
  the office typing a non-zero **base** amount (decision 19 keeps this). So
  *"we email you to confirm your appointment"* still describes one of two live
  branches. **The finding stands; its mechanism was wrong.** Adopt the proposed
  rewording, which is branch-agnostic and better regardless.
- **R3-BL-5 is REDUCED to one true objection.** *"when we call"* and *"never
  after the visit"* are not falsified by a payment link, because there is no
  payment link for travel. But **"never after the visit" is still an
  unenforceable promise about the client's own conduct that the client has not
  made**, and that alone is enough to strike it. → client question.
- ⇄ **R3-CQ-3 is ALREADY ANSWERED and comes off the client list.** *"Travel
  fee: phone, or payment link?"* — **phone, outside the software, enforced in
  code at `review.ts:246`.** Decision 8 + BK-51 settled it.

## 🔴 CONFIRMED BLOCKERS — verified against the code

### R3-BL-2 · Deleting `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` drops a CLIENT REQUIREMENT out of every email

**Verified:** `booking-email.ts` imports `ASSESSMENT_TIER_NAMES` and renders
only `tierNameOf` (`:462`, `:1155`, `:1166`, `:1213`). **It never renders
`ASSESSMENT_TIER_DESCRIPTIONS`** — that constant renders on exactly one surface,
`BookingForm.svelte:1178`.

So the **3–5 business-day lab turnaround** — a **client requirement of
2026-08-18**, recorded in two constants' comments as *"one fact, two places it
must appear, and they may not drift into two different promises"* — reaches the
emails **only** through `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS`. Delete the array and the `sketch`
customer gets a tier *name* in the message they keep and **no turnaround
statement anywhere in it.**

⚠️ **§5a's claim that the fact is "PRESERVED VERBATIM in intent" is FALSE as
written** — it preserves the render that survives and deletes the one that
carries it into email. Same deletion also drops BK-27 Q4's *"each tier includes
the ones below"* clause, which BK-36's first draft dropped and plan review
restored.

⇄ **CORRECTION TO THE PLAN: do NOT delete `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS`. Strip the FIGURES
from it.** The sentences minus the dollar amounts stay true, keep both client
requirements, and kill the free-deliverables implicature **better than silence
does** — silence is what created the implicature in the first place.

### R3-BL-3 · Deleting `FEE_TERMS_REFUND` removes the homepage card's ONLY cancellation instruction

**Verified:** `booking-copy.ts:509` — `FEE_TERMS_REFUND[4]` **is** `CANCEL_LINE`
(*"To cancel or reschedule, call or text …"*), and the array's comment records
why: *"the terms block has to be self-contained on the homepage card, where no
closing line exists."* `CANCEL_LINE` renders independently in
`booking-email.ts` (`:574`, `:627`, `:771`, `:804`), `booking-ics.ts:266` and
`booking-admin-notify.ts` — **but on `ContactSection.astro`, `book.astro` and
the island the terms box is its only render.**

R2-SF4 pinned the surviving **email** render and stopped there. **Deleting the
array leaves the homepage card explaining how to book and nothing about how to
cancel.** → `CANCEL_LINE` becomes the closing line of the new terms block on all
three web surfaces.

### R3-BL-4 · Four spellings of one claim, and a pin cannot be written for a claim with no shape

*"The on-site assessment is free"* sits inches above
`ASSESSMENT_TIER_NAMES.report` = *"Assessment + written report"*, so the reader
composes **assessment = free → this option = assessment + report → the report
comes with the free thing.** §5a's note *fights* that sentence instead of
removing it. The ticket correctly leaves the tier NAMES alone, so **the fix must
be in the free claim.**

⇄ **ONE CANONICAL PHRASE, AND THE NOUN IS "VISIT".** The drafts currently carry
four spellings — *"The on-site assessment is free"*, *"The assessment visit is
free, whichever you pick"*, *"about 30 minutes, free"*, *"Free on-site
assessment"*. **§1's coupling pin has to match all four.** Pinning a claim shape
is only possible if the claim has a shape.

### R3-BL-6 · The ack label is thin consent — and the honest fix is a fact that is already true

The *"above"* reference form is kept correctly (pinned, and the box still
renders above the checkbox). But the amount is stated nowhere, the terms are
new, and the office can mint a link for any amount.

**The reviewer found the sentence that makes it honest, and it is TRUE TODAY:**
`booking-payment.ts:374` uses `mode: 'payment'` with **no `setup_future_usage`
and no stored customer** — **nothing can be taken without the customer opening a
link and paying it themselves.** Adopt:

- new terms item: *"We never take money without telling you the amount first —
  any charge is a link you choose to pay."*
- label → *"I understand the assessment terms above, including that a travel fee
  may apply and that I will be told the amount before anyone comes out."*

   about free while `/book/` does. §1 strikes the ban and asserts nothing.

12. ⚠ **Decision 19 does not repair what it claims to.** Putting more terms into

## 🔴 E1 · DECISION 19 CLOSED A BLOCKER THAT IS STILL OPEN — see the withdrawal above

## 🔴 B2 · `verify-booking-pricing.ts:69-88` CANNOT BE DELETED AS A BLOCK

**§4 said the `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` zip could go because its subject is gone. That
is true of the copy half and FALSE of the engine half.** Inside the same
`forEach`:

```ts
check(
  formatCents(tierDefaultCents(tier)) === `${expected[tier]}.00`,
  `the ${tier} default … matches the copy's ${expected[tier]} to the cent`,

```

**Verified: `:84` is the ONLY absolute-value assertion on `tierDefaultCents` in
the repo.** The other three uses (`:107`, `:119`, `:126`) are all **relative**.
**Delete the block and `TIER_PRICE_CENTS` can drift `39900 → 3990` with
`verify-booking-pricing.ts` fully green** — directly contradicting this ticket's
own boundary decision to *keep `booking-pricing.ts` intact and keep
`verify-booking-pricing.ts` running against it.*

It also destroys a pin **bought with a recorded defect**: the comment above it
records that `"$399.90".startsWith("$399")` was true, so *"the one assertion
whose entire job is price drift passed while the table charged 90 cents more
than the copy states. The cents are the part a typo lands in."*

⇄ **CORRECTED, per BK-44's make-the-fixture-honest rule:** keep `expected` and
the `forEach`; delete **only** the two `copy.includes(...)` checks and the
length zip, and drop the `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` import. **Assertion count 42 → 35,
not 32 — log the expected drop**, per §4's own count rule.

The reviewer wrote the working regex pair and confirmed §2's drafts match both

- **A1 — the chosen scope is VACUOUS for the surfaces §2 puts the claim on.**
  §1 scoped to `booking-copy.ts` + `dist/` and excluded render files. **But §2
  writes *"Free on-site assessment"* as an INLINE LITERAL** into `book.astro:94`
  and `ContactSection.astro:287` — both inline today. **Under §2's own copy the
  free claim does not live in `booking-copy.ts` at all** except
  `ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE`. *"This is R2's criticism reproduced in mirror image."*
  ⇄ **Scope must be derived from where §2's FINAL copy lands, not from where
  today's claims live.** Inline sites outside scope: `book.astro:94`, `:36-37`
  (+JSON-LD `:49`), `ContactSection.astro:287`, `BookingForm.svelte:780-782`,
  `[service].astro:79`.
- **A2 — whole-file co-occurrence on `dist/` is not proximity.** On
  `dist/client/index.html` the claim is the eyebrow and the qualifier is
  hundreds of lines away in another component. **Move the terms box into the
  `showForm` arm — the edit `verify-cutover.ts:440-453` exists to catch, and
  which its own comment records ALREADY SHIPPED GREEN ONCE — and the coupling
  pin stays green** on a homepage claiming an unqualified free assessment.
  → do the proximity half **at source** on the render files; keep `dist/` as a
  whole-file **presence** backstop, and say so in the comment.
- **A3 — on the `.js` arm the verdict is a Vite artifact.** Whether
  `ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE` and its qualifier land in one chunk is a bundler
  decision nobody reviews; `:1108-1118` already records a filename-based pin
  that matched **zero** strings while reading like coverage.
- **A4 — the pin is shaped for the word "free" and the claim has other
  grammars — BK-48 verbatim.** `NO_CHARGE_LINE` (*"There is no charge for this
  visit"*) and `QUOTE_TIMING_NOTE` (*"Nothing is charged now"*) are **both free
  claims matching neither shape**, and `verify-booking-email.ts:377-379`
  **already owns the grammar list** (`/nothing is charged|not charged|no
  charge|nothing to pay/i`). A later editor rewording to *"No charge for your
  visit"* **turns the pin off silently — quiet, not red.**
- **A5 — and it is still not a positive twin.** Nothing asserts the free claim
  is *present*: delete the eyebrow entirely and every gate is green. Add the
  real twin over the built pages.

## 🔴 B1 · THREE ROWS NAMED IN ROUND 1 VANISHED FROM THE REVISION

*"A row that disappears between two versions of a plan is a decision nobody
made."* — `verify-cutover.ts:571-579` (`QUOTE_HEADING` pinned present, its only
render inside the deleted block); `verify-booking-email.ts:742-745` (the `$0`
message's `!/\$[\d,]+\.\d{2}/`, **whose justifying comment cites
`FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` printing "$399 + GST"** — and §2 puts a travel-fee sentence
onto that same $0 message); and **R1-D5 entirely** (`mustNotCarry` built from
three symbols §2 deletes, plus the `slice(0,2)` index addressing).

## 🔴 C1 · THE "KEEP" VERDICT IS RIGHT; THE REASONING IS FALSE FOR ONE OF THE TWO PAGES

§3 merged both pages into one sentence. **`/book/confirmed/` is gated** — by
`planConfirmationRender` (`booking-confirmation.ts:154-173`), which redirects to
`/book/` without a session id. **`/book/payment-cancelled/` is FULLY STATIC** —
no island, no session read, no redirect — and its own header says so:
*"every word of it is true for anyone who arrives."* **Anyone who types or
shares that URL sees "the payment link in your approval email" unconditionally.**
Both stay (deleting 404s a live `cancel_url`), but §3's shared reasoning is
false for the static one, **and the reasoning is what a flip-back reader
re-derives from.**

Also recorded: `/book/confirmed/`'s gate is a **shape check the module itself
calls "A BAR, NOT A PROOF"** — `?session_id=cs_test_aaaaaaaa` renders `PAID_*`
**and fires the Ads conversion** for anyone. Unchanged by BK-53, but §3's *"a
visitor reaches either page only when a real payment genuinely exists"* is not
what the code says.

## 🔴 D1/D2 · THE PICKER — one good verdict, two unaddressed reds

✅ **`assessment_tier` still submits; the 422 incident does not recur.** Verified:
the radios sit **outside** both `{#if}` blocks; only the price `<span>` is
inside. §5's deletion is safe.

- **D1 — `QUOTE_HEADING` is pinned present** (`verify-cutover.ts:571-579`) and
  its only render is inside the block §5 deletes. Red on implementation, in no

- **D2 — `AFTER_HOURS_NOTE` is incoherent, TRIPLE-pinned and unaddressed.**

  ⚠ **And if it goes, `tierQuotes` loses its last reader: `tsconfig.json`
  extends `astro/tsconfigs/strict`, which does NOT enable `noUnusedLocals`** —
  `verify-cutover.ts:558-559` records orphaned imports raising no error when the
  fieldset was once deleted. **Pin their absence; do not rely on svelte-check.**

**C2** the `PAYMENT_CANCELLED_LEAD` exclusion is **unimplementable at whole-file

# 🔁 PLAN REVISION 2 — 2026-09-05. Written against all 16 blockers and decisions 17–19.

**Every case enumerated under the old plan has been re-derived against decisions
17–19**, per CLAUDE.md's rule-rewrite trap. Verdicts that CHANGED are marked ⇄.

## 1 · The pin polarity — resolving R1-B2, the structural blocker

BK-29's four bans exist because *"free assessment"* was **false under the credit
model**. Decision 17 makes it **true for the visit** — and decision 17a makes it
**conditional**, because a travel fee may follow. So neither keeping the ban nor
simply deleting it is right:

- **Keep the ban** → the ticket cannot ship its own copy. Build stays red.
- **Delete the ban** → nothing then stops a surface claiming an *unconditional*
  free assessment, which is false the moment a travel fee applies. That is the
  pre-prepay claim's exact shape, re-armed in the opposite direction.

### ⇄ THE REPLACEMENT IS A **COUPLING PIN**, NOT A BAN AND NOT A PERMISSION

> **On any surface that claims the assessment is free, the travel-fee qualifier
> must also be present.** The claim and its qualifier travel together or the
> gate is red.

This is the **positive twin** CLAUDE.md requires, and it is the only pin shape
that survives a flip back: under a future paid model the free claim simply
stops appearing and the pin goes quiet, rather than needing to be re-inverted.
It also directly answers R1-D8 — the twins **can** now be written, because the
coupling defines them.

**Scope, merging both reviewers' conflicting fixes.** R2 is right that widening
`CLAIM_SURFACES` to render files is near-vacuous: `verify-cutover.ts:735-746`
reads **each file's own source**, and the claims live in `booking-copy.ts`.
R1 is right that the ban list is incomplete. **Therefore both, in two places:**

| Scan | Why |
| --- | --- |
| **`src/lib/booking-copy.ts`** | where the claims actually live — catches them before a render site is even chosen |
| **the built `dist/` output** | how `BANNED_SHAPES` (`:1056-1071`) already works, and how **BK-29 found the claim surviving in `dist/` after a green source sweep** |

Adding the five render files is **not** part of the fix. Recorded so a later
reader does not re-add them as an oversight.

**⇄ `verify-cutover.ts:470-475` (`!/no obligation/i`) is STRUCK, not inverted.**
It was true under the credit model and is false now. Whether *"no obligation"*
goes back **on** is W20/W21's call and a user decision — this ticket removes the
prohibition and asserts nothing in its place.

## 2 · The replacement copy — DRAFTED FOR APPROVAL, not adopted

⚠️ **NOTHING BELOW SHIPS WITHOUT THE USER'S SIGN-OFF.** Client-facing wording is
a user decision per CLAUDE.md; these are drafts to react to, so the ask is a
yes/no rather than a blank page. **The travel-fee AMOUNT is deliberately absent**
— W22: existence needs no decision, the number does.

### The terms box — replaces INTRO / ITEMS / PAYMENT / REFUND / CREDIT

`FEE_TERMS_HEADING` (*"Assessment terms"*) **stays** — the checkbox makes a
claim about it. Three items, one per true term under decision 17:

1. *"The on-site assessment is free. It takes about 30 minutes."*
2. *"If you are outside our usual service area, a travel fee may apply. We tell
   you the amount when we call to confirm — never after the visit."*
3. *"Any report, estimate, or restoration work is quoted separately — at the end
   of your visit or after it."*

**`FEE_TERMS_ACK_LABEL`** ⇄ *"I understand the assessment terms above, including
that a travel fee may apply."* Keeps the reference form (so the "above" markup
claim and its pin survive) and surfaces the one thing that can cost money.

### The other surfaces

| Surface | Draft |
| --- | --- |
| `book.astro:94` + `ContactSection.astro:287` eyebrow | *"Free on-site assessment"* |
| `book.astro:36-37` meta description **and JSON-LD** (R1-A3/R2-SF1) | *"Book a free on-site restoration assessment in Edmonton. About 30 minutes. A travel fee may apply outside our service area — we confirm it before we come."* |
| `FEE_TERMS_INTRO` (`:368`) | *"We review every request before confirming it. Once we approve yours, we email you to confirm your appointment and send the calendar invite."* |
| `RECEIVED_NEXT_STEPS[1]` | *"If we can take it, we email you to confirm your appointment."* |
| `RECEIVED_NEXT_STEPS[2]` | *"Your calendar invite comes with that email."* |
| `QUOTE_TIMING_NOTE` (`:613`) | *"Nothing is charged now. We review your request and email you to confirm."* |
| `llms.txt.ts:54` | *"Pick a time for an on-site assessment — about 30 minutes, free. Booking online sends a request: the office reviews it, and if approved the customer gets a confirmation email and a calendar invite. A travel fee may apply outside the usual service area, confirmed before the visit. Any report, estimate or restoration work is quoted separately. This is the way to request a quote."* |
| `FEE_TERMS_CREDIT` (`:525`) | **DELETED** — there is no credit; the work is quoted separately |

## 3 · ⇄ TWO "OPEN QUESTIONS" NOW CLOSE THEMSELVES — and both close as KEEP AS-IS

**R2-SF2 `/book/payment-cancelled/` and R2-SF3 `/book/confirmed/`.** Both were
flagged as carrying payment copy that survives the flip. **Re-derived: their
copy is TRUE where it renders.**

`cancel_url` is set **only** by `createCheckoutSession` (`booking-payment.ts:384`),
which is reached **only** when `totalCents > 0` (`review.ts:329` routes `=== 0`
to `approveFree`). Likewise `/book/confirmed/` is the Stripe **success** redirect.
**So a visitor reaches either page only when a real payment genuinely exists** —
`PAYMENT_CANCELLED_LEAD`'s *"the payment link in your email"* and `PAID_LEAD`'s
*"what you paid"* are accurate on arrival, not stale.

**Both routes KEPT, both strings KEPT.** Deleting the route would 404 the
`cancel_url` of any session open at deploy time (R1-D3). ⚠ **`PAYMENT_CANCELLED_LEAD`
must still come OFF the payment-link ban list's scope**, or a true sentence
turns the gate red — an instance of the derived-pin trap, caught before it was
written.

## 4 · The reddened assertions — R1-B1, classified before anything is touched

Per CLAUDE.md (BK-44): **establish whether each arm relied on the old behaviour
BEFORE touching the rule. Make the FIXTURE honest; never loosen the rule to
quiet the suite.** Three classes, and they get different treatment:

| Class | Arms | Treatment |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Asserted a claim this ticket legitimately removes** | `verify-booking-email.ts:246-254`, `:291-296`, `:304`/`:311`/`:321`, `:339-349`, `:374`, `:424`; `verify-cutover.ts:350-357`, `:364-372`, `:396-416`, `:441-456`, `:536-545`, `:610-620` | **Rewrite to assert the REPLACEMENT.** Never delete — a deleted assertion is an unpinned claim |
| **Asserted structure that still holds** | `:525-547` (`carries`), `:590-600` (the three `formatCents` renders) | Re-point at the new constants. ⚠ **`:590-600` depends on the picker decision below** |
| **Cannot survive as written** | `verify-booking-pricing.ts:10, 69-88` — imports `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` and zips it to `ASSESSMENT_TIERS` | ⇄ **The "keep it running against `booking-pricing.ts`" row is CORRECTED:** the *engine* pins stay; the **`FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` zip is deleted**, because the copy it checked no longer exists. Deleting a check whose subject is gone is not loosening |

**R2-SF4 — `FEE_TERMS_REFUND[4] === CANCEL_LINE`.** Deleting the array silently
removes **one of the two `CANCEL_LINE` renders** in every customer email.
**Positively pin the survivor** at `booking-email.ts:574`/`:627` so it stops
being an accidental one.

**R1-B3 — the emptied-array hazard.** If a constant is **emptied** rather than
deleted, `for (const line of carries)` runs **zero iterations and the suite
stays green having asserted nothing** — the crashed-script trap in a new body.
⚠ **Rule for this ticket: delete constants, never empty them. And the red-first
log records the summary line's ASSERTION COUNT, not just the exit code**
(the count moved 1509 → 1513 → 1540 across BK-51; a drop is the signal).

## 5 · ⇄ THE TIER PICKER — decision 17's consequence, and it is not copy

`BookingForm.svelte:1171-1213` renders `{formatCents(...)} + GST` beside every
radio plus Base/GST/Total (R1-A1). Under decision 17 **those prices are gone**,
and the deliverables they described are **quoted separately, not included**.

**The picker itself SURVIVES** — decision 15, deliberately, and reversing that
here would be a rule rewrite of its own. What changes:

- **the three `formatCents` renders and the whole quote block are deleted**, and
  `verify-cutover.ts:590-600`, which positively *requires* them, is rewritten to
  assert their **absence** plus the presence of the tier labels;
- ⚠ **`ASSESSMENT_TIER_DESCRIPTIONS` (`:580-587`) must stop implying the report,
  the sketch and the lab work come with the free visit.** Prices off + these
  descriptions unchanged = the implicature survives the deletion. **This is the
  fee-terms trap relocated, and it is the single subtlest item in the ticket.**
  Drafts needed; user sign-off required.
- ⚠ **`verify-cutover.ts:556-565` records the picker being deleted with four
  scripts green and the funnel 100% dead on a 422 for `assessment_tier`.**
  Any edit here keeps a **positive** pin that the picker still submits.

## 5a · ⇄ THE TIER PICKER COPY — DRAFTED. *(the implicature fix, §5's subtlest item)*

**The problem in one line:** delete the prices and leave
`ASSESSMENT_TIER_DESCRIPTIONS` alone, and *"Everything above, plus a written
cause-of-loss report…"* now reads as **three things included with a free
visit.** Nothing false was written; the false reading is created by removing the
prices next to it. **That is the fee-terms trap relocated** — and it is exactly
the shape CLAUDE.md's copy-inventory trap describes, a claim carried by a
sentence rather than by the constant anyone would grep for.

**Fix at two levels — a group note that states the rule once, and per-tier lines
that stop implying inclusion.** The group note is preferred over repeating the
caveat three times: one sentence, one pin, one place to change on a flip back.

**🆕 `ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE`** *(new constant, sits under the legend)*
> *"The assessment visit is free, whichever you pick. Anything beyond it — a
> report, an estimate, a sketch — we quote after we have seen the damage."*

**`ASSESSMENT_TIER_LEGEND`** ⇄ *"Choose your assessment"* → **"What do you think
you need?"** — the picker is now an indication that helps the office prepare,
not a purchase. Decision 15 kept the control; decision 17 changed what choosing
means.

| Tier | Draft description |
| --- | --- |
| `standard` | *"A technician walks the affected areas and writes up the scope of the damage."* **(unchanged — already true and already free)** |
| `report` | *"The visit, plus a written cause-of-loss report and a repair estimate — the documentation your adjuster works from if you are filing a claim. We quote the report after the visit."* |
| `sketch` | *"The visit, plus a report and a measured sketch of the affected areas — quoted after the visit. Where lab samples are taken, results take 3–5 business days."* |

⚠️ **The lab-turnaround sentence is PRESERVED VERBATIM in intent.** It is a
**client requirement from 2026-08-18**, not drafting, and its rationale still
holds word for word: *"documentation they expected on the day is a complaint
rather than a deliverable."* Under decision 17 it matters **more**, not less —
the customer is now also waiting on a quote. **Do not drop it while editing
around it.**

⚠️ **`ASSESSMENT_TIER_NAMES` is left ALONE** — *"Assessment + written report"*
describes scope, not price, and is true under decision 17. Recorded so a later
sweep does not rewrite it for symmetry.

**Pins:** `ASSESSMENT_TIER_NOTE` gets a positive pin (present, and rendered
above the radios); each description gets a negative pin on
`/\bEverything above\b/i`, the exact phrase that carries the inclusion
implicature — **pinning the claim shape, not the constant name.**

## 6 · Sequencing — R2-B4, and it inverts the ticket's own headline

**BK-53 is built ON `bk51-gated`. BK-51 + BK-53 ship in ONE push.**
*"GATE 1 blocking BK-51"* reads as *BK-53 first*; deployed alone on `main` it
gives a priceless site with an Approve screen still pre-filling $418.95
(`[id].astro:505-512`, verified) and minting Checkout Sessions. **Never
separately.**

**The drain step — R2-B5.** ⇄ The step named only `pending_review`, the **benign**
direction (decision 16 accepts those becoming free). Corrected to three statuses,
with the probe written out per BK-48 rather than an instruction to check:

```sql
SELECT id, status, payment_due_at, total_amount_cents
FROM appointments
WHERE status IN ('pending_review','approved_awaiting_payment','payment_expired')
ORDER BY status, slot_start;
```

`approved_awaiting_payment` is the one that hurts: those rows hold a live
Checkout Session and a deadline, a copy deploy touches none of them, and the
customer pays days later for a visit the site now calls free.
**Its output is pasted into this ticket before the push.**

## 7 · What still needs the user

1. **Sign-off on §2's drafted copy** (or edits).
2. **`ASSESSMENT_TIER_DESCRIPTIONS` rewrite** — §5, drafts to follow.
3. **The travel-fee amount**, or confirmation we disclose existence without a
   number (W22 says existence needs no decision).
4. **Does *"no obligation"* go back on?** — §1, W20/W21's call.

# ⛔ PLAN REVIEW ROUND 1 — 2026-09-05. NOT APPROVED.

**11 blockers / 8 should-fix / 4 nits.** The three most consequential are
recorded here in full; the reviewer's numbering is preserved. **The surface
table below is superseded in the places these findings name — read this section
first.** Every finding quoted here was re-verified against the code before
being written down.

## 🔴 B2 — THE GATE FORBIDS THE COPY THIS TICKET EXISTS TO SHIP

**This is the structural finding and it reshapes the ticket.** Four live
assertions ban the claim "the assessment is free":

| Pin | Scope |
| --- | --- |
| `verify-cutover.ts:512-517` | `!/free\s+assessment/i` on the rendered templates of `book.astro` and `ContactSection.astro` |
| `verify-cutover.ts:1056-1071` | `BANNED_SHAPES` — `/\bfree[^.<>]{0,40}?assessments?\b/i` **and** the predicative `/\bassessments?\b[^.<>]{0,24}?\b(?:is\|are\|'s)\s+free\b/i` — over **every built `.html` and `.js` in `dist/`** |
| `verify-cutover.ts:1188-1193` | *"llms.txt does not tell an assistant the assessment is free"* |
| `verify-cutover.ts:470-475` | `!/no obligation/i` on the same two files — **true** under the free model, so the ban is now wrong |

**Verified by direct read.** `npm run verify:cutover` fails the build on any
page or bundle that says the assessment is free. **That is the entire purpose
of this ticket.** BK-29 ("remove the free-assessment claim site-wide") wrote
those pins under the paid model; the model has flipped and every case
enumerated under the old rule must be re-derived — **CLAUDE.md's rule-rewrite
trap, at repository scale.** The ticket's "Do NOT touch" list names three pins
to preserve and is silent on all four of these.

**A plan that cannot produce a green gate for its own replacement copy is not a
plan.** Inverting these pins is now a first-class part of BK-53, not cleanup.

## 🔴 B1 — ~20 EXISTING ASSERTIONS GO RED, AND THE PLAN NAMED NONE

CLAUDE.md: *"A fix that turns EXISTING assertions red is a question about the
assertions, not about the fix."* The plan's only pin work was "add banned
regexes, widen the file list."

`verify-booking-email.ts` positively pins the content being deleted — `:246-254`
(`figures.includes('399'/'699'/'1,199'/'GST')`, its own comment calling itself
*"the reason a silent price deletion cannot ship"*), `:291-296` (the credit),
`:304`/`:311`/`:321` (the three refund windows), `:339-349` (refund block
ORDER), `:374` (*"`FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT` names the payment link"*), `:424`
(`AFTER_HOURS_NOTE` states the multiplier), `:525-547` (`carries` over
{request, confirmed} × {html, text}), `:638`, `:740-750`.
`verify-cutover.ts` — `:350-357`, `:364-372`, `:396-416`, `:441-456`,
`:536-545`, `:575-581`, `:590-600`, `:610-620`.

⚠️ **And `verify-booking-pricing.ts:10, 69-88` imports `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` and
zips it to `ASSESSMENT_TIERS`.** The "delete to a boundary" table says *"keep
`verify-booking-pricing.ts` running against it"* — **it will not typecheck** if
`FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` is deleted. That row is not achievable as written.

**Per CLAUDE.md (BK-44):** establish for each reddened arm whether it was
relying on the old behaviour BEFORE touching it. Do not loosen a rule to
quiet a suite.

## 🔴 D1 — THE TICKET CONTRADICTS ITSELF ON `FEE_TERMS_REFUND`

- Line ~260: **"Delete `FEE_TERMS_REFUND` outright"** — *"a refund policy a
  config flip can switch on is a policy nobody reviewed."*
- Lines ~219-221: **row-gate the terms block on `settled.totalCents > 0`** so a
  paid-era resend receives the terms it was actually charged under.

**Both cannot hold.** Delete the constants and the `> 0` branch has nothing to
render, so a Resend on a paid-era row (`#36`, `cs_live_`, $628.43) silently
drops the refund policy from the record of a completed purchase — which
`booking-email.ts:499-510` says in as many words must not happen. Two decisions
made under different predicates, neither re-derived against the other.
**Rule-rewrite trap. Policy question — escalate, do not let an implementer
pick.**

## The other eight blockers, in brief

- **A1 — the surface table's single worst error.** `BookingForm.svelte:1171-1213`
  renders `{formatCents(tierQuotes[tier].baseCents)} + GST` beside **every
  radio**, plus Base/GST/Total at `:1196-1213`, fed by `assessmentQuote`.
  **The table below asserts `TIER_PRICE_CENTS` is "kept intact and
  unreferenced." It is referenced, live, on the public form every web booking
  passes through** — the largest price surface on the site. Verified by direct
  read. `verify-cutover.ts:590-600` positively *requires* those three renders.
- **A2 — every REQUEST email prices the assessment.** `booking-email.ts:459-463`
  → `assessmentSummary` `:441-450`: `settled` is null on a request, so it
  renders *"You chose: Standard assessment — $399.00 + GST ($418.95 total)"*
  plus *"— weekend rate, 1.5x"*. Computed from `assessmentQuote`, **not** from
  `FEE_TERMS_ITEMS`, so the table's `booking-email.ts:525` row does not cover it.
- **A3 — `/book/`'s meta description, OG tags and JSON-LD carry the credit
  claim.** `book.astro:36-37` — *"the fee is credited back in full when you go
  ahead with the work"* — reaching `<meta name="description">`, `og:description`,
  `twitter:description` and the `WebPage` schema at `:49`. **This falsifies the
  ticket's own "structured data is fine, there is no price anywhere in it"**,
  which was reached by grepping `priceRange|priceCurrency|lowPrice|highPrice` —
  a constant-shaped grep for a claim stated in words. The trap, again.
- **A4 — `FEE_TERMS_CREDIT` (`booking-copy.ts:525`) is absent from claim 4**,
  though it renders on four surfaces: `ContactSection.astro:357`,
  `BookingForm.svelte:1125`, `book.astro:149`, `booking-email.ts:533`/`:610`.
- **A5 — `book.astro:146` renders `FEE_TERMS_REFUND`**; claim 3 names only
  ContactSection, the island and the email. `/book/` is the indexed page.
- **D2 — `settled.totalCents > 0` is a two-state gate on a three-state value.**
  `settledAmountsOf` returns **null** for the thirteen migration-008 pre-prepay
  `confirmed` rows. Written literally it does not typecheck; written
  `settled?.totalCents > 0` it routes those rows into the FREE branch. Must be
  three-state (`null` / `0` / `>0`).
- **D3 — `/book/payment-cancelled/` IS still reachable, and my premise was
  wrong.** Verified: `review.ts:329` routes `if (totalCents === 0)` to
  `approveFree`, **else** `createCheckoutSession` (`:487`), whose `cancel_url`
  is `BOOKING_PAYMENT_CANCELLED_PATH` (`booking-config.ts:307`, `:348`).
  **Free is the DEFAULT, not the only path.** The open question below —
  *"whether the page should keep existing"* — **has an answer: it must**, or
  the live paid path gets a 404 as its Stripe cancel URL. Its copy needs
  correcting, not deleting.
- **D4 — nothing in the plan says what a CHARGED customer is told.** Same root
  as D3. Because the office can still enter an amount, deleting the price, the
  refund promise and the payment-link sentence does not retire those claims —
  it leaves a **live money path the site now says nothing about.** A customer
  approved at $399 gets a link and a deadline having ticked a box whose terms
  no longer mention refunds, the deadline or the credit. **This is the
  strongest argument against the copy-only classification.**

## Should-fix, condensed

**A6** `AFTER_HOURS_NOTE` (`booking-copy.ts:597`) missing from claim 6, pinned
in three places · **A7** the four `src/pages/[service].astro` landing pages say
*"no obligation"* at `:79`, and the table never names that file · **A8**
`PAYMENT_CANCELLED_HELP_LINE` (`:780`) is a second payment claim on that route ·
**B3** emptying an array rather than deleting it makes `verify-booking-email.ts:525-547`
loop zero times and **stay green having asserted nothing** — twin of the
crashed-script trap; **the red-first log must assert the summary line's
assertion COUNT, not only the exit code** · **D5** `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT` is
index-addressed in three files (`booking-email.ts:1150` `slice(0,2)`,
`verify-booking-email.ts:538-540`, `:556-560`) — shrinking it silently changes
what `slice` returns · **D6** `terms_acked_at` snapshot named as a risk then
not scheduled; either do it here or record it in ROADMAP Known traps with an
owner, per the out-of-scope rule · **D7** the drain step states the answer, not
the probe — write the literal SQL · **D8** the positive twins cannot be written
until B2 is decided.

## ✅ What the review CONFIRMED

- **The `booking-copy.ts:134-192` boundary is correct.** All six symbols are
  consumed only by `booking-admin-notify.ts` (`:489-491`, `:517`, `:546-547`),
  every call gated on a real refund outcome on the row. None renders on a
  forward-looking surface. **Keep them.**
- **The pin-gap claims (i) and (ii) are both confirmed by inspection** —
  `CLAIM_SURFACES` is five files, and no existing shape matches the payment-link,
  price or credit claim. They are simply not the whole gap.
- **D9 — `TIER_PRICE_CENTS` poses no arithmetic hazard.** No division; integer
  arithmetic throughout. Keeping `booking-pricing.ts` intact is safe.

## ⚖️ TIER — "copy-only, plain Reviewed" DOES NOT HOLD. Adversarial pass required.

The trigger is a property of the change, not of the ticket's description of
itself. Four independent clears: **(1)** it touches what the appointment
insert's consent attests to — `FEE_TERMS_ACK_LABEL` feeds the box whose tick is
stamped at `booking-commit.ts:91-99`, and `verify-cutover.ts:520-545` exists
because a review once deleted that box with every gate green and `terms_acked_at`
stamped against terms never shown; **(2)** it moves money claims on a live send
path (`resend.ts`, and D1/D2 show the conceded scope was drawn before the
contradiction was found); **(3)** it removes the live price display from the
public form (A1) — `verify-cutover.ts:556-565` records the picker being deleted
with four scripts green and the funnel 100% dead on a 422; **(4)** its main
artifact IS the pin set, and BK-51 measured 11/11 adversarial breaks green in a
suite nobody was rewriting.

**Recorded decision: BK-53 takes a full adversarial pass.** The ticket's
earlier "plain Reviewed" line is struck.

# ⛔ PLAN REVIEW ROUND 2 — 2026-09-05. Customer / irreversibility / policy lane.

**5 blockers, 5 should-fix, 5 nits, 7 user decisions.** Verdict: *"The plan's
shape — delete to a boundary, keep the pricing engine, snapshot nothing new in
the DB — is right; its enumeration and its one write-path fix are not."*

## 🔴 R2-B4 — BK-53 ALONE ON `main` MANUFACTURES THE DRAIN STEP'S OWN WORST CASE

**Verified by direct read.** `main`'s `[id].astro:505-512` still computes
`suggestedQuote` from `assessmentQuote(tier, service, slot)`. **BK-51's $0
default exists only on `bk51-gated`.**

So a BK-53 deployed by itself yields a live site with **no price, no refund
policy and no payment-link claim anywhere a customer can read**, while the
Approve screen still pre-fills $418.95, mints a Checkout Session
(`booking-payment.ts:383-384`) and sends `APPROVED_*` with a link and a
deadline. That is *"a customer who read free gets a payment link"* — the drain
step's own worst case — applied to **every new booking** rather than to a small
backlog.

**The ticket calls itself "GATE 1 blocking BK-51's push", which reads as
BK-53 FIRST. That reading is dangerous.** The ticket names no branch at all
while `ROADMAP.md:92` says `git checkout bk51-gated  # to work on BK-51 / BK-53`.
**Corrected: BK-53 is built ON `bk51-gated`, and BK-51 + BK-53 ship in ONE
push, never separately.**

## 🔴 R2-B1 — `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT[0]` SHIPS ON THE ONLY MESSAGE THE FREE CUSTOMER KEEPS

`booking-copy.ts:460` — *"**Those are our standard rates.** What you pay depends
on the service you need and the time you pick — the booking form shows that
price before you send your request, and the approval email states the amount we
will charge."*

`feeTermsPaymentFor` hands `[0]` **and** `[1]` to the **confirmed** arm. Delete
`FEE_TERMS_ITEMS` and the free customer's confirmation reads: *Assessment
terms* → *"Those are our standard rates."* → *"There is no charge for this
visit."* **"Those" refers to nothing that was printed** — and three further
clauses are independently false (no price on the form, no approval email on the
free path, no "amount we will charge").

`[3]` (`:463`) meanwhile threatens, on `/book/`, the island and both request
emails, to **release the slot over a payment nobody will ask for.**

⚠️ **The ticket's own §"Re-derive from `feeTermsPaymentFor`" says in as many
words that fixing `[2]` alone ships `[0]` on the kept message — and the table
below it still omits `[0]` and `[3]`.** Reviewers work from the table.

## 🔴 R2-B2 — CLAIM 7's FIX HAS AN UNSTATED POLARITY, AND NEITHER POLARITY WORKS

Gate the terms block **on** `totalCents > 0` and it renders **exactly for the
paid-era row** — `#36` receives free-mode terms, which is the defect verbatim.
**Invert** it and the paid-era row gets **no terms at all**, contradicting
`booking-email.ts:501-511` (*"terms that vanish from the record of a completed
purchase are terms nobody can point at afterwards"*).

**Root cause: once `FEE_TERMS_REFUND` is deleted outright — this ticket's own
boundary decision — no text remains in the tree that is true for a paid-era
row. A gate SELECTS; it cannot RESTORE.** Only two coherent resolutions, and
the ticket must pick one:

- **(a)** snapshot the rendered terms (or a `terms_version`) at insert
  (`booking-commit.ts:91-99`) and render `resend.ts:106` from the snapshot; or
- **(b)** suppress the terms block for pre-flip rows and state plainly that the
  terms record for paid-era rows is now git-history-only.

⚠️ **`ROADMAP.md`'s entry for this same defect specifies the gate AND the
snapshot together; this ticket kept the gate and demoted the snapshot to a
parenthetical.** A row that disappeared between two versions of a plan is a
decision nobody made — **rule-rewrite trap.** And this is the one item the
ticket scopes its adversarial pass to, so it is the one spec that must not be
ambiguous.

## 🔴 R2-B3 — THE REQUIRED CHECKBOX SURVIVES OVER AN EMPTY BOX

`FEE_TERMS_ACK_LABEL` → `BookingForm.svelte:1222`, required at
`booking-form.ts:276`, mirrored at `booking-payload.ts:316`, stamped
`terms_acked_at` (`booking-commit.ts:99`), shown to the office
(`[id].astro:1018`).

Remove INTRO/ITEMS/PAYMENT/REFUND/CREDIT and all three terms boxes render
`FEE_TERMS_HEADING` (*"Assessment terms"*) over an **empty `<ul>` and zero
paragraphs**. The label's *"the assessment terms **above**"* then points at a
bordered box containing a heading and white space — **and the checkbox is still
REQUIRED.** A customer at step 3 is stopped by a mandatory tick accepting terms
that are not there, on a funnel already at ~1 booking/fortnight, in the month
the free offer is being judged. **User decision — do not resolve in code.**

## 🔴 R2-B5 — THE DRAIN STEP NAMES THE BENIGN STATUS AND MISSES THE HARMFUL ONE

- `pending_review` → approved post-flip becomes a **free** booking. Decision 16
  explicitly accepts that cost. **This is the benign direction, and it is the
  only one the step names.**
- **`approved_awaiting_payment` is the one that hurts:** those rows already
  hold an emailed payment link, a `payment_due_at` and a **live Checkout
  Session**. A copy deploy touches none of them. The customer pays days later
  for a visit the site now calls free, and `markPaid` confirms it.
- `payment_expired` is a third: post-flip that customer receives
  `PAYMENT_EXPIRED_LEAD` — *"was not paid for by the deadline, so we have
  released it"* — **about a free assessment.**

The step also names no query, no owner and no evidence. Per BK-48: **write the
probe, and require its output pasted into the ticket before the push.**

**The fix must instead scan `booking-copy.ts` itself and/or the built `dist/`

`priceRange|priceCurrency|lowPrice|highPrice` — asking what the pin is NAMED,
not what it MATCHES; BK-48's lesson firing inside the paragraph that certifies

loads** · **SF2** close the open question as **KEEP `/book/payment-cancelled/`**
— fix `booking-copy.ts:778`, keep the route · **SF3** `/book/confirmed/`'s

## Lane C — the boundary, judged

- **Keep `booking-pricing.ts` intact and unreferenced — agreed without
  reservation.** Pure computation, no customer-facing text, 42 pins free to
  keep green, and it is exactly what a fourth pricing model needs.
- **Delete the forward-looking refund promise — agreed. Scope — disagreed.**
  The ticket then leans on a `resend.ts` gate to cover the historical case, and
  after deletion there is nothing left to gate to (R2-B2). Separate them, and
  **take the terms snapshot: under decision 16 it is the single
  highest-value item in this ticket, because it is the one structure that
  survives an arbitrary number of flips** — and it is currently a parenthetical.
- 🔻 **`ASSESSMENT_PRICES_SHOWN = false` — DEAD WEIGHT AS SPECIFIED.** A boolean
  never read in a conditional is a comment with type-checking. It rots, or
  worse someone reads it as a switch, flips it, and gets a half-restored paid
  mode with prices back and **no refund policy and no payment terms** — exactly
  the un-reviewed policy this ticket refuses to let a config flip create.
  **Better: make the terms function itself the anchor** — `assessmentTermsBlock()`
  is greppable, is *called*, and cannot rot into an unused export. If the
  constant stays, **assert it** (exists, is `false`, referenced at N sites).
  *"An unasserted grep anchor is the thing this repo's trap log keeps paying
  for."*

## Lane D — what the site now promises, and where it misleads

Reduces to: a ~30-minute on-site visit, a written scope, no charge for the
visit, a reviewed request, a held slot. **Nothing about the report, lab work,
travel, or what happens if money is ever asked for.**

- **Gap 1 — free VISIT vs free REPORT, the sharpest.** Decision 15 keeps the
  tier picker, and `ASSESSMENT_TIER_DESCRIPTIONS` (`:580-587`) promises a
  cause-of-loss report, a repair estimate, a measured sketch and **lab samples
  at 3–5 business days**. Prices deleted, those become three *free* options,
  the top one carrying a **real per-sample cost**. Decision 16's accepted "one
  or two bad free bookings" was priced against a technician's hour, **not
  against a lab invoice.**
- **Gap 2 — the manual non-zero path has no terms behind it at all.**
  `APPROVED_*` (`booking-email.ts:738-771`) carries no fee terms and no refund
  policy — it never had to, because the terms lived on the booking surfaces.
  After BK-53 they live nowhere. A customer charged $250 by hand has no
  published refund policy, an acknowledged box that said nothing about money,
  and `APPROVED_DEADLINE_NOTE` disclosing the slot-release consequence **for
  the first time in the email asking for money.**
- **Gap 3 — "no charge" reads wider than intended.** `NO_CHARGE_LINE` becomes
  the only money sentence in the confirmed email, and deleting
  `FEE_TERMS_CREDIT` removes the last statement that the **restoration work**
  is billed at all.
- **Gap 4 — copy that implies an amount without naming one:**
  `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT[0]`, `book.astro:37`, `QUOTE_TIMING_NOTE` (*"Nothing is
  charged **now**"*), `PAID_LEAD` (*"what you paid"*), `PAYMENT_CANCELLED_LEAD`,
  `booking-payment.ts:288`, and `DECLINED_LEAD`'s *"Nothing has been charged"*
  — which implies a world in which something could have been.

## ⚠️ THE PIN GAP — `verify-cutover.ts`'s `CLAIM_SURFACES` IS FIVE FILES

```
BookingForm.svelte · ContactSection.astro · book.astro · contact.astro · llms.txt.ts
```

**Every one of these carries a claim above and is scanned by NOTHING:**

- `src/components/BookingConfirmation.svelte` — renders `RECEIVED_NEXT_STEPS`
- `src/pages/book/received.astro` — `<BookingConfirmation variant="received">`
- `src/pages/book/confirmed.astro` — same component
- 🆕 `src/pages/book/payment-cancelled.astro` — `PAYMENT_CANCELLED_LEAD`
- `src/lib/booking-email.ts` — **23 references to the fee-terms family**, i.e.
  every confirmation and request email, pinned by no claim-shape check at all

And the existing shapes match **none** of claims 2, 3 or 4:
`BOOKED_CLAIM_SHAPES` is about *instant confirmation*; `ONSITE_PAYMENT_SHAPE`
is about *paying on site*. **A payment link existing is a third claim, and it
is unpinned on every surface.** This is BK-48's lesson exactly — *ask what a
pin matches, not what it is named.*

**Therefore the gate is:** add the payment-link, price and credit shapes to
`CLAIM_SURFACES` as new banned regexes, **and widen `CLAIM_SURFACES` to the
five unscanned files above** — the ban list and the file list are two separate
holes and fixing one alone fixes nothing. Per CLAUDE.md, every negative pin
gets a **positive twin**: assert the replacement sentence is present, not only
that the old one is absent.

**Still to derive before implementation:** the replacement copy for each claim
(client-facing wording — a user question, not a code one), and whether
`/book/payment-cancelled/` should keep existing at all once the default path
mints no session.

**Fix by grepping the CLAIM** — `/payment link|payment goes through|secure payment/i`
across `src/` — and add that shape to `verify-cutover.ts`'s `CLAIM_SURFACES` as a
new banned regex. `BOOKED_CLAIM_SHAPES` and `ONSITE_PAYMENT_SHAPE` match
**neither**: they are about instant confirmation and on-site payment, not about a
payment link existing.

⚠ **Re-derive from `feeTermsPaymentFor`, NOT from the constant.**
`booking-email.ts:1150-1151` slices `FEE_TERMS_PAYMENT.slice(0,2)` for a
*confirmed* message — so `[2]` reaches the **request** email and the pages, while
the **confirmed** email carries `[0]` and `[1]` beside `NO_CHARGE_LINE`. Fixing
`[2]` alone ships `[0]` on the message the customer keeps.

> ## ⚠️ THE ADS QUESTION — CORRECTED 2026-09-02, AND THE FIRST VERSION OF THIS
> ## BOX WAS WRONG IN A WAY THAT WOULD HAVE COST MONEY
>
> **What this box used to say:** that a free approval mints no Stripe session, so
> the booking conversion can never fire, leaving a ~CA$59/day campaign with
> *"ZERO conversion input"* — and it recommended **pausing the campaign** as
> *"free, instant"*.
>
> **The mechanism is right. The conclusion was wrong, twice.**
>
> **1 · The account is NOT going dark.** `CONVERSION-2026-08-31.md` §3A's own
> conversion table: **`Website call` (30) and `Calls from ads` (15) are Active and
> last fired Aug 31** — together **45 of August's 66 conversions**. Both live in
> `Analytics.astro:29-35` and are untouched by BK-51/52/53. Meanwhile
> `Assessment booked` has been **Misconfigured since Aug 20** and `Request quote`
> since **Aug 11**. **So this ticket does not kill a live signal; it makes an
> already-dead one structurally dead.**
>
> **2 · Pausing is NOT free.** Google documents *"campaign status"* as a
> learning-disrupting change and *"Reactivated"* as a trigger for re-entering
> Learning. Whether the accumulated model survives a pause is **not stated in
> Google's docs** — do not assume either way. And nothing removes a dark
> conversion action before **13 months**; archiving is reversible.
>
> **The corrected position:**
>
> | Option | Verdict |
> | --- | --- |
> | **Leave it running** on the phone conversions that are actually firing | **Default.** Costs nothing, loses nothing |
> | **Pause it** | A legitimate **budget** decision, not a tracking one. Costs relearning on restart |
> | **Re-point the conversion to `/book/received/`** | ⛔ **The one to avoid.** You pay 1–2 conversion cycles now and again on any flip back — on the primary bidding signal, in the month the offer is being judged, with Google documenting nothing about historical comparability |
>
> **This is T6, orphaned when T2 was struck.** It is a decision for the client,
> taken separately from this code batch. **It does not block BK-53.**

- **The weekend 1.5× removal** (decision 7) — the multiplier plus **six surfaces
  that state it in words**. Grep the *claim*, not `AFTER_HOURS_NOTE`. Includes
  `[id].astro:1801-1802`, which annotates a field now reading `0.00`.
- **W20/W21 are BACKWARDS and must be re-derived here, not shipped as written** —
  they strike *"no obligation"*, which is the **true and best** claim for the
  in-zone majority under decision 1.

## ⚠️ A LIVE CROSS-MODE DEFECT THIS TICKET MUST FIX, NOT INHERIT

**Emails already sent are frozen. The Resend button is not.**

`resend.ts:106` calls `planForAppointment(appointment, …, now)` — it re-renders
**today's constants** against a **stored row**. The numeric half is safe:
`paymentClaim` and `paidAmounts` (`booking-email.ts:412-413`, `:441`) derive from
`settled`, so a paid row still says "paid in full" and a $0 row still says
`NO_CHARGE_LINE`.

**The prose half is not gated at all.** `booking-email.ts:522-533` reads
`FEE_TERMS_ITEMS`, `feeTermsPaymentFor()`, `FEE_TERMS_REFUND` and
`FEE_TERMS_CREDIT` as **unconditional constants**.

**So the moment this ticket ships:** the office clicks Resend on a paid-era
confirmed booking — production has real ones, `#36` was a live `cs_live_` charge
of $628.43 — and that customer receives **free-mode terms, including a refund
policy that contradicts what they were actually charged.** Reachable today:
`resend.ts` is gated only on `status === 'confirmed'` plus a real email address.

**The fix is not a flag.** Row-gate the terms block on `settled.totalCents > 0`,
exactly the way `paymentClaim` at `:441` already is — the correct pattern is
eight lines above the defect. **Red-first it with a paid-era row.**

*(`terms_acked_at` records THAT the box was ticked, never WHAT it said. Once the
text changes there is no reconstruction but git history plus `created_at`. Not
made worse by this ticket, and a second reason the terms must be row-derived.)*

## Inherited defects, owned by this ticket

1. `booking-payment.ts:288` — Stripe line *"Round trip beyond the free radius"*,
   now unreachable, describes the retired per-km rule.
2. `TRAVEL_FEE_CENTS_PER_KM` / `TRAVEL_FEE_FREE_RADIUS_KM` — dead exports naming
   a **retired** rule. BK-51 pins nothing in `src/` reads them.
3. `booking-copy.ts:437` — justifies live customer copy with *"an editable amount
   **and a travel-fee field at approval**"*. False since BK-51.
4. **`appointmentMoney`'s `estimate` arm renders the phantom ladder on the
   office's detail page** — `$399.00 + GST · Estimate, nothing approved yet`.
   Owned jointly with BK-52; decide which ticket removes it.

## DELETE TO A BOUNDARY, NOT TO NOTHING

**Established 2026-09-02 by two design passes, against decision 16 (the client is
experimenting and may flip again).** This ticket as first drafted deletes the
pricing machinery permanently. That is the wrong shape for a model on its third
revision.

**The finding that decides it:** the money engine is **already mode-free.**
`approve()` computes a total and routes on `totalCents === 0`; BK-51 reduced the
entire free-vs-paid difference on that path to **one expression** — what the
amount field defaults to. The DB stores *amounts*, never a mode. A mixed
population of free and paid rows is already the design. **The only real
difference between the two models is static prose.**

**So: do the deletion, but delete to a boundary.**

| Do | Instead of |
| --- | --- |
| Keep `booking-pricing.ts` **intact and unreferenced**, and keep `verify-booking-pricing.ts` running against it | Deleting the tier ladder, the GST helper and 42 pinned checks |
| Route the terms prose through **one function with exactly one implementation** | Eight loose `FEE_TERMS_*` constants read directly by five surfaces and four email renders |
| Use **one named constant** (`ASSESSMENT_PRICES_SHOWN = false`) at the suppression sites, as a grep anchor | Deleting the sites and leaving nothing to grep for |
| **Delete `FEE_TERMS_REFUND` outright** | Gating it — a refund policy a config flip can switch on is a policy nobody reviewed |

**Do NOT write the paid-mode prose you don't need.** Sentences are what rot and
what a reviewer must weigh. **Do NOT add a mode column to the DB** — rows are
self-describing today (`payment_method='none'` + `paid_amount_cents=0`
discriminates cleanly, forever), and a mode column would make the one currently
reversible-by-construction thing permanent.

**The honest counter-argument, recorded because it nearly won:** a flag does not
reduce the work, it relocates it and doubles the state space of every assertion —
in a suite that BK-51 proved cannot catch a re-introduced defect under a
different spelling. **The resolution: the reversible thing is the STRUCTURE, not
the sentences.** What this buys is the elimination of archaeology — *"where did
this render, what did it say, what pinned it"* — which is the cost this repo's
own trap log says it keeps paying.

## What a flip back CANNOT undo

Classified 2026-09-02. Most fears here are unfounded; two are real.

**Fine — verified, stop worrying about these:**
- **DB rows.** Nothing stores a mode. A flip-back reads free rows correctly
  forever.
- **Structured data.** There is **no price anywhere in it** — `seo.ts`'s
  `hasOfferCatalog` carries `itemOffered` only; a grep for `priceRange`,
  `priceCurrency`, `lowPrice`, `highPrice` across `src/` returns **zero**.
  Nothing to remove, nothing to restore, no rich-result loss.
- **Stripe sitting unused 6–12 weeks.** Keys do not expire at that horizon (the
  only documented inactivity rule is 180 days and is scoped to payouts). No
  Products/Prices exist to archive — `booking-payment.ts` builds `price_data`
  inline. `verify-stripe-webhook.ts` stays green at 91/91 throughout.
- **The Google index.** `/book/` is in the sitemap; copy changes re-crawl
  normally.

**Real, and the second one is the sharp one:**
- **`/llms.txt` distribution.** `robots.txt` allows GPTBot, ClaudeBot,
  Google-Extended and CCBot. Training corpora do not retract. *Mild in practice*
  — the paid version is already in those corpora, so a flip-back is the
  better-supported direction.
- **⚠️ NOTHING SNAPSHOTS WHICH TERMS EACH BOOKING ACCEPTED.** `terms_acked_at`
  is a bare timestamp. Combined with the Resend defect above, **a flip in either
  direction rewrites the evidence of what past customers agreed to.** This is the
  single most expensive thing to get wrong here, it cannot be fixed
  retroactively, and it is cheap now: store a `terms_version` (or the rendered
  block) at insert in `booking-commit.ts`, and make `resend.ts` render from the
  snapshot.

## The drain step — required at ANY flip, in either direction

Before deploying, `pending_review` rows submitted under the **old** copy must be
resolved under the **old** rules. Otherwise a customer who read *"free"* gets a
payment link, or a customer holding a live Checkout Session with a
`payment_due_at` pays for what the site now calls free. Production population is
currently tiny — but **the check must be MADE at deploy time, not assumed.**

## Do NOT touch

`verify-cutover.ts`'s `BOOKED_CLAIM_SHAPES` and `ONSITE_PAYMENT_SHAPE`, and
`verify-booking-email.ts:365`'s ban on *"paid at the visit"*. **All are still
TRUE under a free assessment** and the temptation to invert them is the
documented wrong edit.
